### [1] [Fusing Perceptual Vision Experts with Multimodal Large Language Models for Explainable Plant Disease Diagnosis: From Benchmark Imagery to Real-World Robotic Field Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24934)
*Ranjan Sapkota,Konstantinos I. Roumeliotis,Pengyao Xie,Nikolaos D. Tselikas,Lirong Xiang,Manoj Karkee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出混合分层多智能体框架（H²MAF），融合EfficientNet-B3和ConvNeXt-Tiny的决策级融合以及开源多模态大语言模型（Gemma 4 E4B和Qwen3.5 4B）的语义仲裁，通过结构化JSON证据实现可解释的植物病害诊断。在14,364张图像（含1,370张测试图）上评估，PlantDoc准确率从63.9%提升至68.5%，Cornell田间数据达到98.9%-99.3%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确的田间植物病害诊断需要可靠融合不确定且冲突的感知证据，解决传统CNN模型在野外条件下因图像质量差、光照变化等因素导致的诊断冲突问题，并提供可解释的诊断结果。

Method: H²MAF框架包含两层：1）决策级融合层：集成EfficientNet-B3和ConvNeXt-Tiny两个CNN模型的输出；2）语义仲裁层：使用开源MLLM（Gemma 4 E4B和Qwen3.5 4B）对CNN冲突结果进行语义仲裁，通过结构化JSON证据生成可解释诊断、风险等级、治疗紧迫性和财务风险暴露。

Result: 在PlantDoc上Gemma将准确率从63.9%提升到68.5%，在41.7%的CNN冲突子集上提升7.6个点；Cornell田间数据集准确率达99.3%和98.9%，仅1.7-4.1%的模型间不一致；Gemma的严重风险错误仅0.14-0.5个点，而Qwen过度标记达3.5-14.4个点。

Conclusion: MLLM仲裁是有前景但需要校准的可解释农业AI方法。Gemma 4 E4B在冲突处理上优于Qwen3.5 4B，但MLLM的校准至关重要，证明了该框架在机器人田间决策支持中的潜力。

Abstract: Accurate field plant disease diagnosis requires reliable fusion of uncertain and conflicting perceptual evidence. We present the Hybrid Hierarchical Multi-Agent Framework (H$^{2}$MAF), combining decision-level fusion of EfficientNet-B3 and ConvNeXt-Tiny with semantic arbitration by open-weight multimodal large language models (MLLMs), Gemma 4 E4B and Qwen3.5 4B, using structured JSON evidence to generate explainable diagnoses, risk levels, treatment urgency, and financial exposure. (H$^{2}$MAF) is evaluated on 14,364 images (1,370 test images) across PlantDoc (2,922 images, 27 classes) and two non-public, continuously captured Cornell robot-acquired field datasets: Stage 2 (20 GB; 4,215 images) and Stage 4 (40 GB; 7,227 images), covering Early Blight, Late Blight, and Septoria Leaf Spot under uncontrolled field conditions. On PlantDoc, Gemma improves accuracy from 63.9% to 68.5%, achieving +7.6 points on the 41.7% CNN-conflict subset. Cornell accuracies reach 99.3% and 98.9%, with only 1.7-4.1% disagreement, demonstrating conflict-dependent MLLM utility. The critical-risk error of gemma is 0.14-0.5 points, whereas Qwen overflags by 3.5-14.4 points. These results establish MLLM arbitration as a promising, yet calibration-dependent, approach for explainable agricultural AI and robotic field decision support. Github Link: https://github.com/Applied-AI-Research-Lab/Explainable-AI-Plant-Disease-Detection

</details>


### [2] [A Lightweight Multimodal Vision-Language Framework for Early-Stage Anatomical Green Fruit Classification in Commercial Orchards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24935)
*Ranjan Sapkota,William Bu,Chen Chen,Yunjun Xu,Manoj Karkee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于TinyCLIP的轻量级多模态视觉-语言框架，用于苹果幼果早期解剖结构的细粒度分类。在600张高分辨率RGB图像数据集上，通过滑动窗口推理策略实现花萼、果体和果柄三类结构的空间热图定位。在NVIDIA T4 GPU上取得0.93的宏观F1分数，并通过ONNX和TensorRT优化实现边缘设备上的毫秒级高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确识别早期苹果幼果的解剖结构（花萼、果体、果柄）对机器人疏果、作物负载管理及精准果园作业至关重要，但现有方法在复杂果园环境中缺乏轻量级且可解释的感知能力。

Method: 采用TinyCLIP作为基础模型构建轻量级多模态框架；将600张RGB图像转换为224x224图像块，分为三类解剖结构；使用领域特定的语言提示词引导多模态对齐；采用步长为112像素的滑动窗口推理策略将块级别预测聚合成空间热图；通过ONNX和TensorRT优化实现边缘部署和INT8量化。

Result: 在NVIDIA T4 GPU上，花萼F1分数为0.95，果体为0.98，果柄为0.85，宏观F1为0.93。模型大小为127-137MB，在Jetson硬件上实现毫秒级推理，INT8量化下保持精度。

Conclusion: 轻量级视觉-语言模型能为自动化幼果分析和未来机器人疏果系统提供可解释且可边缘部署的感知能力，代码已公开。

Abstract: Accurate identification of early-stage apple fruitlet anatomical structures, including the calyx, fruitlet body, and peduncle, is essential for robotic thinning, crop-load management, and other precision orchard operations. This study presents a lightweight multimodal vision-language framework that adapts TinyCLIP for fine-grained fruitlet anatomy classification in complex orchard environments. A dataset of 600 high-resolution RGB images collected from Scilate and Scifresh apple orchards was converted into 224 x 224 image patches and annotated for three anatomical classes. Domain-specific language prompts, such as ``a photo of a class,'' were used to guide multimodal alignment between orchard imagery and horticultural structures. A sliding-window inference strategy with a stride of 112 pixels aggregates patch-level predictions into spatial heatmaps, enabling interpretable whole-image localization of fruitlet components relevant to robotic thinning. Patch-level evaluation on an NVIDIA T4 GPU achieved F1-scores of 0.95 for calyx, 0.98 for fruitlet, and 0.85 for peduncle, with a macro-F1 score of 0.93. Deployment-oriented optimization using ONNX and TensorRT enabled efficient inference on NVIDIA Jetson hardware, preserved accuracy under INT8 quantization, and supported model sizes of approximately 127-137 MB with millisecond-level patch inference. These results demonstrate that lightweight vision-language models can provide interpretable and edge-deployable perception for automated fruitlet analysis and future robotic thinning systems. The source code and implementation details are publicly available at https://github.com/WilliamBu1/A-Lightweight-Vision-Language-Model-for-Early-Stage-Fruitlet-Classification-in-Apple-Orchards.

</details>


### [3] [Synergising Local Geo-Environmental Characteristics with Spatial Context for Enhancing Landslide Susceptibility Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24956)
*Yusen Cheng,Lei Fan,Qinfeng Zhu,Cheng Zhang,Yangyang Li,Ron Mahabir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为LGSCF（Local-Geo and Spatial Context Fusion）的融合策略，将滑坡点的地理环境特征与其空间上下文通过特征调制机制相结合，用于改进滑坡易发性制图（LSM）。该策略被集成到九种不同的CNN架构中，在台湾南投县仁爱乡和信义乡约2644 km²的研究区域内进行了验证，结果显示LGSCF模型在F1分数（最高87.09%）和AUC值（最高0.9472）上均优于原始模型，且生成的易发性图将已知滑坡更准确地集中在"极高"易发区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动的滑坡易发性制图方法存在两类数据表示的问题：基于像素的模型只关注目标滑坡点的地理环境特征，忽略了周围环境的影响；而基于图像块的模型虽能纳入空间上下文，但可能包含与目标滑坡位置空间相关性较弱的像素。因此需要一种既能融合局部地理特征又能有效利用空间上下文且避免弱相关像素干扰的策略。

Method: 提出LGSCF（Local-Geo and Spatial Context Fusion）策略，通过特征级调制机制将滑坡点的局部地理环境特征与其对应的空间上下文进行协同融合。该策略被集成到多个代表性CNN架构中，构建了九种不同的LGSCF模型，利用5332个滑坡样本和等量的非滑坡样本在台湾南投县进行研究验证。

Result: LGSCF模型在各项指标上均一致优于原始CNN模型，F1分数最高达到87.09%，AUC值最高达到0.9472。此外，LGSCF模型生成的滑坡易发性地图能更准确地将已知滑坡集中在"极高"易发区域，且误分类更少。

Conclusion: 所提出的融合策略能够显著提升滑坡易发性制图的准确性，有效弥补了像素级模型和图像块级模型各自的不足，为数据驱动的滑坡易发性制图提供了一种更优的技术方案。

Abstract: Data-driven methods are widely used in landslide susceptibility mapping (LSM) because they can effectively model the complex relationships between landslides and geo-environmental conditions. Existing data-driven approaches generally follow two types of data representations. Pixel-based models focus solely on the geo-environmental characteristics of a specific landslide but neglect the influence of its surrounding environment. Patch-based models incorporate surrounding spatial context but may include pixels with weak or no spatial relevance to the target landslide location. To address this limitation, this study proposes a Local-Geo and Spatial Context Fusion (LGSCF) strategy, which synergises the geo-environmental characteristics of landslide points with their corresponding spatial context through a feature-wise modulation mechanism. We tested the LGSCF strategy by integrating it into several representative convolutional neural network (CNN) architectures, creating nine different LGSCF-based models. The study area covers approximately 2644 km2 across Jenai and Sinyi Townships in Nantou County, Taiwan, and the dataset comprises 5332 landslide samples and an equal number of non-landslide samples. The results show that LGSCF-based models consistently outperform their original versions, achieving F1-scores up to 87.09% and AUC values up to 0.9472. Furthermore, the susceptibility maps produced by LGSCF-based models show that known landslides are more accurately concentrated in "very high" susceptibility zones with fewer misclassifications. These findings demonstrate that our fusion strategy can significantly improve the accuracy of landslide susceptibility mapping.

</details>


### [4] [Targeting the Attention Heads Behind Object Hallucination in LLaVA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24966)
*Armaan Sandhu,Abhilasha Senapati,Hima Kammachi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对视觉语言模型（如LLaVA-1.5-7B）在图像描述中产生物体幻觉的问题，提出了一种从可解释性诊断到定向修复的完整流程：通过注意力头排名筛选出32个关键头，并施加切片LoRA适配器和推理时接地控制器两种干预，将CHAIRs从0.370降至0.230、CHAIRi从0.156降至0.096。通过随机头对照实验确认了头选择的有效性而非LoRA容量作用，并揭示该方法在降低幻觉的同时付出了物体召回率下降（0.78→0.70）的代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在生成图像描述时频繁幻觉出图像中不存在的物体，这一问题严重影响模型可靠性与可解释性。作者希望探究：能否利用可解释性诊断来定位这一失效模式，并据此进行有针对性的修复，同时严格测量修复措施究竟改变了什么行为。

Method: 首先按注意力头在幻觉物体词周围的图像注意力下降程度对头进行排序，再通过消融候选头并测量幻觉token对数概率变化筛选出32个关键头。在此基础上实施两种针对这些头的干预：头切片LoRA适配器（微调）和推理时接地控制器（解码约束）。同时设计随机头LoRA作为对照实验，并分析不同解码预算下的CHAIR变化。

Result: 在400张COCO测试图像上，组合方法将CHAIRs从0.370降至0.230，CHAIRi从0.156降至0.096（p<0.001）。随机头对照在200张控制集上与基线无显著差异，证明是头选择起效而非LoRA容量。CHAIR降低在不同解码预算下持续存在且随预算增大（64 token时23%降至128 token时58%），排除截断伪影。但物体召回率从0.78降至0.70。

Conclusion: 本文建立了从可解释性诊断到干预的完整研究范式，证明诊断信号能定位具有真实、非随机效力的干预位点。更重要的是，作者以行为画像而非单一分数的方式报告了干预效果：它在降低幻觉的同时也付出了召回率代价且输出更短更保守，提示未来工作需要在高保真与高召回之间寻求更精细的平衡。

Abstract: Vision-language models such as LLaVA-1.5-7B often hallucinate objects absent from the image when generating captions. We ask whether an interpretability diagnosis of this failure can guide a targeted fix, and we measure what that fix actually changes. We rank attention heads by how much their image attention drops around hallucinated object words, then screen the shortlist by ablating candidate heads and measuring the change in hallucination-token log probability, yielding a 32-head set. We restrict two interventions to these heads: a head-sliced LoRA adapter and an inference-time grounding controller. On 400 held-out COCO images, the combined method lowers CHAIRs (the fraction of captions with a hallucinated object) from 0.370 to 0.230 and CHAIRi (the fraction of hallucinated object mentions) from 0.156 to 0.096 (p < 0.001, paired sign-flip tests). Two controls sharpen attribution. A random-head LoRA control, matched layer-for-layer and trained identically, performs no better than the matched baseline on a separate 200-image control split, supporting the role of head selection rather than LoRA capacity. Under fixed decoding budgets, the CHAIR reduction persists and grows with budget (23% at 64 tokens to 58% at 128), arguing against a pure max-token or truncation artifact, although the method remains shorter and more conservative. The resulting behavior reduces unsupported object mentions while also lowering object recall (0.78 to 0.70). We present a diagnosis-to-intervention pipeline for object hallucination, and, more importantly, a controlled account of what acting on the diagnostic signal actually does: it localizes intervention sites with real, non-random leverage, reported as a behavioral profile rather than a single score.

</details>


### [5] [SHIFT-LLM: Distribution Shift Correction in Depth-Pruned LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25068)
*Ali Bahri,Hang Li,Hongliang Li,Zhitang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SHIFT-LLM是一种无需训练的剪枝后修正框架，通过在深度剪枝位置插入线性残差适配器（LRA），用闭式最小二乘回归校准来近似被剪除Transformer块产生的残差更新，以缓解层移除导致的数据分布失配问题，在五个模型家族、六个层选择标准和七个零样本基准上持续恢复精度损失，最高提升+15.7个点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度剪枝虽然能降低大语言模型的推理成本，但移除Transformer块会破坏下游层所期望的隐藏状态分布，导致显著的精度损失。现有方法缺乏一种高效且无需训练的后剪枝修正手段来缓解这种分布失配。

Method: 在每个剪枝位置插入线性残差适配器（LRA），保留原始残差块的恒等路径并添加轻量仿射残差修正；该修正通过小型留出集上的闭式最小二乘回归进行校准，无需梯度计算；LRA支持低秩分解和跨连续剪枝层的精确合并以进一步压缩，并可自然结合参数高效微调。

Result: 在五个模型家族、六个层选择标准和七个零样本基准上，SHIFT-LLM在多数配置下持续恢复深度剪枝造成的精度损失，在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上最高提升+15.7个点，仅需数百个校准样本且无需梯度计算。

Conclusion: SHIFT-LLM通过轻量的闭式回归适配器有效缓解了深度剪枝带来的分布失配问题，是一种高效、可扩展且能与参数高效微调互补的后剪枝修正方法。

Abstract: Depth pruning removes entire Transformer blocks to reduce the inference cost of large language models, but disrupts the hidden-state distributions expected by downstream layers, leading to significant accuracy loss. We introduce SHIFT-LLM, a training-free post-pruning correction framework that inserts a Linear Residual Adapter (LRA) at each pruning site. Each LRA preserves the identity pathway of the original residual block and adds a lightweight affine residual correction. This correction is calibrated via closed-form least-squares regression on a small held-out set, without gradient computation, to approximate the missing residual update produced by the pruned block. Together with the preserved identity pathway, the resulting LRA output approximates the hidden state produced by the original block, thereby mitigating the distributional mismatch introduced by layer removal while avoiding the expensive attention and feed-forward computations of the removed blocks. The resulting LRAs support low-rank factorization and exact merging across consecutive pruned layers for additional compression, and combine naturally with parameter-efficient fine-tuning for further recovery beyond fine-tuning the pruned model alone. Experiments on five model families, six layer-selection criteria, and seven zero-shot benchmarks show that SHIFT-LLM consistently recovers accuracy lost to depth pruning across most configurations, achieving gains up to +15.7 points on Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct while requiring only a few hundred calibration samples and no gradient computation.

</details>


### [6] [RefLAM: A Reference-Grounded Line Annotation Pipeline for Historical Arabic Manuscripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25140)
*Mohamed Guechaoui,Mohamed Diaa Zellagui,Souleyman Chaib,Sahraoui Dhelim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RefLAM，一个将手稿页面图像和转录文本转化为经过验证的行级标注的流水线，通过"置信度100规则"提供可证明的正确性保证，大幅提升阿拉伯文字手写文本识别训练数据的标注效率。作者还发布了AraMS-28k数据集并验证了其在下游HTR训练中的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的阿拉伯语手写文本识别（HTR）行级训练数据构建方法要么依赖全人工标注（不可扩展），要么依赖自动OCR到参考文本的对齐方法，但尚未扩展到多脚本、双区域（正文+页边距）手稿布局，且缺乏可证明的正确性保证。

Method: RefLAM由三部分组成：1) 深度学习页面分割模型；2) 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）进行结构化OCR；3) 忽略变音符号的模糊对齐引擎，将每条OCR行对接到参考文本的连续片段，并给出[0,100]范围的字符级置信度。核心是"Confidence-100规则"：满分即证明归一化字符串逐字符相同。

Result: 在7本完全页面验证的书籍中达到75倍人工标注吞吐量提升（3000行/小时 vs 40行/小时）；一周内处理7本额外书籍保留16,533条置信度100的正文行；发布AraMS-28k数据集（14本书、3,043页、27,971条正文+629条页边距行标注）；微调Muharaf预训练基线模型后报告CER结果证实其实用性。

Conclusion: RefLAM通过可证明的正确性保证实现了"分流式"标注——审阅者可快速信任高分结果，将注意力集中在不确定的对齐上，有效解决了历史阿拉伯语手稿行级标注的规模化难题，并为下游HTR模型训练提供了高质量数据资源。

Abstract: Existing approaches to building line-level Arabic handwritten-text-recognition (HTR) training data either rely on fully manual annotation, which does not scale, or on automatic OCR-to-reference alignment methods not yet extended to multi-script, two-zone (main-plus-margin) manuscript layouts with a provable correctness guarantee. We present RefLAM (Reference-grounded Line Annotation for Manuscripts), a pipeline converting manuscript page images and clean transcriptions into validated, line-level ground truth without sacrificing human oversight. RefLAM couples a deep-learning page-segmentation model with a multimodal large language model (MLLM) for structured OCR and a diacritic-agnostic fuzzy alignment engine that grounds each OCR line in a contiguous span of the reference text, with a character-level confidence score in $[0,100]$. A perfect score is provably equivalent to character-for-character identity of the normalised strings (the Confidence-100 rule), verified with no counterexample across the released corpus. A reviewer can thus trust a perfect score, confirming most lines at a glance rather than retyping them, so annotation becomes triaged, with attention concentrated on uncertain alignments. Across 7 fully page-validated books we measured a 75$\times$ throughput gain over manual annotation (3,000 vs. 40 lines/hr); applying the same guarantee to 7 further books, we retained 16,533 confidence-100 main-text lines within one week, excluding sub-100 lines rather than manually correcting them. Using RefLAM, we release AraMS-28k: 14 historical Arabic manuscript books, 3,043 pages, and 27,971 main-text and 629 margin-line annotations with bounding boxes, layout labels, and insertion anchors for 191 margin entries (30.4%). We also finetune Muharaf-pretrained baselines (including HATFormer) on AraMS-28k and report CER results confirming its practical utility for downstream HTR training.

</details>


### [7] [Can You Trust Frozen Hematology Foundation Models under Acquisition Shift?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25148)
*Jai Kumar Sharma,Peeyush Tapadiya*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究审计了15个冻结编码器（包括血液学、病理学和通用视觉领域）在四个单细胞采集域上的表现，发现域内线性探针精度近乎饱和（macro-F1 0.98-0.997），但跨数据集精度下降34-72%且排名重排。校准同样崩溃：域内ECE仅0.004，域外达0.35。提出无训练方法CBR（类平衡重标准化）改善目标先验场景均值，并指出血液学FM基准需联合审计精度、校准、暴露和类先验鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 血液学基础模型的嵌入在域内WBC任务上已达饱和精度，但临床部署要求跨扫描仪、站点、染色和制备流程的可靠性，现有基准未能系统评估这些模型的鲁棒性和校准性能。

Method: 审计15个冻结编码器（血液学、病理学、通用视觉），沿精度鲁棒性和校准两个轴，在四个公共单细胞采集域上进行评估；分析线性探针与1-NN检索的排名迁移；审计预训练数据暴露问题；提出CBR（类平衡重标准化）作为无训练的伪标签平衡特征归一化方法。

Result: 域内macro-F1饱和（0.98-0.997），但跨数据集下降34-72%；域内最佳DinoBloom-L在最大偏移目标上排名第10/15。1-NN检索比源拟合线性头更稳定（中位ρ 0.65 vs 0.45），但两者均不能普适预测目标鲁棒性。校准崩溃（域内ECE 0.004→域外0.35），温度缩放迁移差。发现MLL23是DinoBloom的内部队列，基准无法隔离暴露与扫描仪偏移。标签自由适配和边际熵模型选择在平衡评估下安全但在真实类先验偏移下失败。CBR改善了所有目标先验场景均值并部分改善校准。

Conclusion: 血液学基础模型基准必须联合审计精度、校准、暴露和类先验鲁棒性四个维度，不能仅依赖域内精度或单一探针评估模型性能。

Abstract: Frozen hematology foundation-model (FM) embeddings reach near-saturated in-domain white-blood-cell (WBC) accuracy, but clinical deployment demands reliability across scanners, sites, stains and preparation pipelines. We audit 15 frozen encoders (hematology, pathology, and general vision) across four public single-cell acquisition domains along two axes: accuracy robustness and calibration. In-domain linear-probe macro-F1 is saturated (0.98-0.997), yet cross-dataset macro-F1 drops 34-72% and rankings re-order: DinoBloom-L, the in-domain best, falls to 10th of 15 on the most-shifted target (MLL23) at the benchmark's shared 224-px input, behind RedDino and several general and pathology encoders. Rank transfer is probe-dependent: 1-NN retrieval is more stable on average than a source-fitted linear head (median $ρ$ 0.65 vs 0.45), but neither probe universally predicts target robustness. Calibration also collapses: source-trained probes are nearly calibrated in-domain (expected calibration error, ECE, 0.004) but confidently wrong off-domain (ECE 0.35), and source-fitted temperature scaling transfers poorly. We further audit pretraining exposure and identify MLL23 as DinoBloom's internal cohort; because DinoBloom's only held-out dataset is also our source domain, this benchmark cannot isolate exposure from scanner-associated shift. Label-free adaptation and marginal-entropy-based model selection appear safe under balanced evaluation but fail under realistic WBC class-prior shift. Class-Balanced Re-standardization (CBR), a training-free pseudo-label-balanced feature normalization, improves all evaluated target-prior scenario means and partially improves calibration, although encoder-level exceptions and residual miscalibration remain. Hematology FM benchmarks must therefore jointly audit accuracy, calibration, exposure, and class-prior robustness.

</details>

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对四种主流音视频同步（AV-sync）指标进行了系统性审计，发现没有单一指标全面胜出：Synchformer/DeSync在时间偏移检测上最强，ImageBind/JavisScore更贴近人工标注代理和内容破坏场景，而AV-Align表现最弱；各指标间一致性极低（Krippendorff α=0.066），简单的融合策略也无法提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AV-sync自动指标被广泛用于音视频生成模型的排序和训练，但作为测量工具本身却很少被严格审计，缺乏统一的可靠性验证。

Method: 建立了一套统一的可靠性审计协议，包括：受控失真单调性测试、预处理敏感性分析、排序不确定性评估、跨指标一致性检验、PEAVS代理一致性检验以及学习融合策略评估。

Result: Synchformer/DeSync在时间偏移追踪上最强（τ=0.84）；ImageBind/JavisScore在人对齐代理（τ=0.20）和内容破坏场景上表现更好；AV-Align是最弱指标；指标间一致性极低（α=0.066），线性和k-NN融合均无改进。

Conclusion: 建议采用Reliability Card（包含指标族细分和置信区间的可靠性卡片）来报告AV-sync结果，而非依赖单一裸同步分数。

Abstract: Automatic AV-sync metrics are widely used to rank and train audio-visual generators, but they are rarely audited as measurement instruments. We jointly audit AV-Align, ImageBind AV-relevance, JavisScore, and Synchformer/DeSync under a common reliability protocol: controlled-distortion monotonicity, preprocessing sensitivity, rank uncertainty, cross-metric agreement, PEAVS-proxy agreement, and learned fusion. The result is an axis split, not a single winner: Synchformer/DeSync is the strongest temporal-offset tracker ($τ=0.84$), ImageBind/JavisScore better match the PEAVS human-aligned proxy ($τ=0.20$) and content-disruption families, and AV-Align is the weakest standalone metric. The metrics mutually disagree (Krippendorff $α=0.066$), and neither linear nor simple $k$-NN fusion improves PEAVS agreement over the best individual metric. We recommend reporting AV-sync as a Reliability Card (metric-family breakdowns with confidence intervals) rather than a single bare synchronization score.

</details>


### [9] [See More, Detect Less? Taming Information Leakage in Multi-View Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25168)
*Shang-Fu Chen,Kuan-Chuan Peng,Jhih-Ciang Wu,Wen-Huang Cheng,Kai-Lung Hua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GLAD框架，首次将视觉基础模型特征与局部和全局跨视角融合用于多视角异常检测。核心发现是跨视角信息泄漏会导致重建型检测器失效，因此必须显式限制传入解码器的信息。GLAD通过MMA模块（线性复杂度局部融合）和OGA模块（对象级全局上下文聚合）实现信息约束，在Real-IAD和MANTA-Tiny数据集上全面超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多视角异常检测方法简单融合多视角信息时，正常视角的线索会传播到解码器，导致解码器忠实地重建异常区域，从而消除检测所依赖的重建差异。这种"跨视角信息泄漏"使得更多跨视角信息反而损害检测性能，因此需要设计一种能够显式限制传入解码器信息的融合机制。

Method: GLAD包含两个核心模块：1) 多视角合并注意力(MMA)模块，在线性复杂度下进行局部跨视角融合，通过可学习的视角重要性权重和token级门控，让每个视角有选择地从其他视角获取细粒度证据；2) 对象引导注意力(OGA)模块，通过温度缩放sigmoid门控将所有视角的类别token聚合成单一对象级表示并广播回patch token，用替换而非残差的方式来保留重建差异。

Result: 在Real-IAD和MANTA-Tiny数据集上，GLAD在样本级、图像级和像素级指标上均优于最先进方法，验证了原则性的信息限制是多视角异常推理的关键。

Conclusion: 跨视角信息泄漏是多视角异常检测中一个此前未被认识的关键失败模式。有效的多视角融合必须显式限制到达解码器的信息量，而有原则的信息约束（而非简单融合更多信息）才是多视角异常推理的关键。

Abstract: In multi-view anomaly detection, more cross-view information can actually hurt. When multiple inspection views are naively fused in a reconstruction-based pipeline, normal cues from intact views propagate to the decoder, which faithfully reconstructs anomalous regions, collapsing the reconstruction gap the detector depends on. We call this failure mode \emph{cross-view information leakage} and show that effective multi-view fusion must explicitly restrict the information reaching the decoder. Building on this insight, we present GLAD(Global-Local Attention Driven framework), the first framework combining vision foundation model features with local and global cross-view fusion for multi-view anomaly detection. The Multi-view Merging Attention (MMA) module performs local cross-view fusion at linear complexity with learnable view importance weighting and token-wise gating, letting each view selectively incorporate fine-grained evidence from other views at $\mathcal{O}(N)$ cost. The Object-Guided Attention (OGA) module captures global context by aggregating class tokens from all views into a single object-level representation and broadcasting it back to patch tokens via temperature-scaled sigmoid gating, replacing the original patch representations rather than adding a residual to preserve the reconstruction gap. Experiments on Real-IAD and MANTA-Tiny show that GLAD outperforms state-of-the-art methods across sample-, image-, and pixel-level metrics, confirming that principled information restriction is key to multi-view anomaly reasoning.

</details>


### [10] [Lowering the Barrier to AI-Driven Inspection: A No-Code Workflow for Automated Structural Defect Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25176)
*Michael Holm,Tanner McElroy,Xinghang Zhang,Guang Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: YOLOEZ是一个开源、基于GUI的端到端YOLO模型应用工具，集数据标注、训练和推理于一体，使无编程经验的用户也能开发高性能缺陷检测模型。实验表明其检测性能优于传统图像处理方法，同时降低了现代CV工具的使用门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构健康监测(SHM)对现代工程至关重要，但现有的CV缺陷检测方法（如CNN和YOLO）虽然在精度和实时性上表现优异，却因需要编程专业知识处理数据标注、模型训练和部署等技术门槛，导致实际应用受限。作者旨在填补这一空白，让非技术用户也能使用先进的AI检测工具。

Method: 开发了YOLOEZ——一个开源、基于图形用户界面（GUI）的工具，将数据标注、模型训练和推理集成到单一界面中，实现无代码开发高性能YOLO模型，同时支持可复现的工作流程。通过与传统图像处理方法和现有软件进行对比评估验证其性能。

Result: 评估结果表明，YOLOEZ在大多数检测指标上优于传统图像处理方法，并且在可用性方面降低了其他现代CV工具存在的采用障碍，兼顾了准确性与可访问性。

Conclusion: YOLOEZ通过结合高精度与易用性，有望推动AI驱动的结构监测在预测性维护、数字孪生和智能结构系统中的更广泛应用。

Abstract: Structural health monitoring (SHM) is essential in modern engineering, providing data for condition-based maintenance, lifecycle assessment, and predictive decision-making. Traditionally, SHM relied on visual inspection to detect defects such as cracks and deformations. Early computer vision (CV) methods, including thresholding, edge detection, and handcrafted features, aimed to automate this process but were highly sensitive to noise, imaging variations, and multiscale defects, limiting their reliability.
  Recent advances in machine learning, particularly convolutional neural networks (CNNs) and You Only Look Once (YOLO), have improved defect detection accuracy and enabled real-time analysis. However, adoption in SHM remains limited due to technical barriers such as data labeling, model training, and deployment, which typically require programming expertise.
  To address this gap, we introduce YOLOEZ, an open-source, GUI-based tool for end-to-end YOLO model application. YOLOEZ integrates data labeling, training, and inference into a single interface, enabling high-performance model development without code while supporting reproducible workflows.
  Evaluation against existing software and classical image processing demonstrates that YOLOEZ not only outperforms traditional methods across most detection metrics, but also lowers adoption barriers present in other modern CV tools. By combining accuracy with accessibility, YOLOEZ facilitates wider use of AI-driven monitoring for predictive maintenance, digital twins, and intelligent structural systems.

</details>


### [11] [Lightweight Machine Learning-Driven Monocular Sidewalk Path Extraction for Embedded Micromobility Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25178)
*Lkhanaajav Mijiddorj,Yang Yan,Tyler Beringer,Bilguunzaya Mijiddorj,Alex N. Ho,Bin Xu,Binbin Weng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向微出行系统的单目视觉人行道路径提取管线，通过三次设计迭代（骨架图基线→距离变换走廊规划→轻量图像空间架构）系统比较了五种路径规划方法。采用SegFormer-B0学生模型配合半监督师生框架训练，IoU达0.946且每帧仅需11.7ms；最终推荐架构以图像空间中点规划为主，全栈在CPU上运行低于50ms/帧，适合嵌入式场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人行道尺度的路径提取需要在紧凑低功耗硬件上、杂乱且地图稀疏的环境中可靠运行感知与规划，现有方法在单片机和微出行系统上缺乏高效且稳健的解决方案。

Method: 采用三次设计迭代从骨架图基线演进到图像空间架构；SegFormer-B0学生模型通过OneFormer Swin-L伪标签的半监督师生框架训练；系统比较BEV域与图像空间域的五种路径规划方法，并评估分割稳定性与模板路径可用性。

Result: 分割IoU从0.758提升至0.946，速度从18.9ms降至11.7ms；图像空间中点规划横向中心误差最低（14.3px）且仅需2.2ms，较BEV距离变换方法提速421倍；时间不稳定度从1.46%降至0.33%，模板路径可用率从73.7%提升至79.3%；全栈CPU运行低于50ms/帧。

Conclusion: BEV单目路径提取在真实场景中脆弱（99.3%帧无有效BEV路径），推荐以图像空间规划为主、BEV仅用于可视化的架构组合，适用于嵌入式行人速度微出行系统。

Abstract: Sidewalk-scale path extraction demands perception and planning that run reliably on compact, low-power hardware in cluttered, map-sparse environments. We present a monocular vision pipeline for sidewalk path extraction in micromobility systems that progresses through three design iterations, from a skeleton-graph baseline through distance-transform corridor planning to a lightweight image-space architecture, and provides a systematic comparison of five path-planning methods across both bird's-eye-view (BEV) and image-space domains. A compact SegFormer-B0 student model, trained with a semi-supervised teacher-student framework using OneFormer Swin-L pseudo-labels, achieves a hand-annotated IoU of 0.946 at 11.7 ms per frame, improving over the baseline checkpoint (IoU 0.758, 18.9 ms). In a controlled planner comparison on 32 hand-labeled frames, image-space midpoint planning achieves the lowest lateral center error (14.3 px) at 2.2 ms, a 421x speedup over BEV distance-transform planning (926.8 ms, 65.0 px center error), while maintaining comparable mask-path alignment (98.5% versus 98.6%). A full-video replay across six campus sequences (22,679 frames) confirms that the improved segmentation reduces temporal instability from 1.46% to 0.33% and increases template-path availability from 73.7% to 79.3%. We further show that BEV-only path extraction is fragile in monocular settings: in one profiled run, 99.3% of frames produced no valid BEV path. The final recommended architecture, image-space midpoint primary, image-space distance-transform fallback, and BEV reserved for visualization, runs the full perception-to-path stack in under 50 ms per frame on CPU, making it suitable for embedded pedestrian-speed micromobility systems.

</details>


### [12] [What Do Medical Vision-Language Models Learn in Radiology? Transfer, Alignment, and Source-Proxy Leakage Under Distribution Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25251)
*Ayoub Louaye Bouaziz,Lokmane Chebouba,Yassine Himeur*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了医学视觉语言模型（VLM）在跨数据集、跨监督模式和跨评估协议下的失效模式，发现单一协议下的表面能力可能掩盖迁移、对齐和捷径相关的缺陷，强调了在分布偏移下对医学VLM进行压力测试评估的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学视觉语言模型在域内表现可靠，但当日志领域、配对监督或评估协议发生变化时会出现失败。作者将其视为表征层面的盲区，与认知智能相关，旨在揭示和系统化度量这些隐藏的失败模式。

Method: 使用NIH ChestXray14和CheXpert进行源域跨数据集视觉迁移与无监督域适应诊断；使用PadChest和OpenI评估多模态对齐（严格配对索引检索）并量化元数据来源代理信息在冻结嵌入中的保留程度。对比自监督视觉初始化与有监督ImageNet初始化，检验对抗性域适应的有效性，并辅以最近邻和Grad-CAM定性分析。

Result: 自监督视觉初始化在NIH到CheXpert迁移上优于有监督ImageNet初始化（匹配ResNet-18对比）；对抗性域适应仅在狭窄范围内有效且随对抗压力增加变得不稳定；多模态精确配对检索在OpenI外部压力测试下表现低下；来源代理信息仍可从学习表征中恢复；定性分析显示临床合理结构但设备密集和假阳性案例仍模糊。

Conclusion: 医学VLM在单一评估协议下表现出的能力可能具有欺骗性，掩盖了迁移、对齐和捷径相关失效模式。研究呼吁在分布偏移条件下对医学VLM进行压力测试评估，辅助架构检查是任务依赖的，不存在通用骨架排名。

Abstract: Medical vision-language models (VLMs) can appear reliable in-domain while failing when acquisition domain, paired supervision, or evaluation protocol changes. We study this failure mode as a representation-level blind spot relevant to epistemic intelligence, without claiming a formal estimator of epistemic uncertainty. Using NIH ChestXray14 and CheXpert, we first isolate source-only cross-dataset visual transfer from unsupervised domain-adaptation diagnostics. Using PadChest and OpenI, we then evaluate multimodal alignment under strict pair-index retrieval and quantify metadata-derived source-proxy information retained in frozen embeddings. Self-supervised visual initialization improves NIH-to-CheXpert transfer over supervised ImageNet initialization in matched ResNet-18 comparisons, whereas adversarial adaptation is useful only in a narrow regime and becomes unstable as adversarial pressure increases. Multimodal exact-pair retrieval remains low under external OpenI stress testing, and source-proxy information remains recoverable from learned representations. Qualitative nearest-neighbor and Grad-CAM analyses show clinically plausible cross-dataset structure and thoracic attention patterns in many cases, while device-heavy and false-positive cases remain ambiguous. Auxiliary architecture checks are task-dependent and do not support a universal backbone ranking. Overall, the study shows that apparent competence under a single protocol can conceal transfer, alignment, and shortcut-related failure modes, motivating stress-tested evaluation of medical VLMs under distribution shift.

</details>


### [13] [OpenCVL: An Open, Diverse, and Large-Scale Dataset for Fine-Grained Cross-View Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25274)
*Zimin Xia,Mubariz Zaffar,Junsheng Fu,Alexandre Alahi,Julian F. P. Kooij*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenCVL，一个大规模、多样化的开放跨视角定位（CVL）数据集，包含来自欧洲四国41个城市的617,388对地面-航拍图像对。该数据集融合高精度传感器数据和野外（in-the-wild）图像，并开发了数据筛选框架来过滤和校正姿态标注，以构建可靠的野外评估数据。实验表明，引入含噪的野外数据能持续提升干净测试集上的CVL模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CVL数据集依赖高精度传感器套件收集数据，限制了图像多样性和可扩展性；虽然野外图像丰富，但其嘈杂的地理标签不适用于可靠评估。这促使作者构建一个大规模、多样化、开放的数据集来弥补这一缺口。

Method: 1) 构建OpenCVL数据集，从允许的平台收集617,388对跨视角图像，覆盖欧洲四国41个城市；2) 训练集混合高精度传感器图像和多样的野外图像；3) 开发数据筛选框架，过滤和校正姿态标注以构建可靠的野外评估数据；4) 包含专门的跨区域和雪天测试集来评估泛化性和鲁棒性。

Result: 使用最先进的CVL模型在OpenCVL上进行实验，结果显示将含噪的野外数据纳入训练能够持续提升模型在干净测试集上的性能，表明利用多样的真实世界图像来扩展CVL是一个有前景的方向。

Conclusion: OpenCVL填补了CVL领域高质量大规模开放数据集的空白，证明了野外含噪数据对提升CVL模型性能的价值，为可扩展、可复现的CVL研究提供了重要基础资源。

Abstract: Fine-grained Cross-View Localization (CVL) estimates the precise position and orientation of a ground-level image by aligning it with geo-referenced aerial imagery, offering a scalable alternative to Global Navigation Satellite Systems (GNSS) in challenging urban environments. Existing datasets rely on data collected with high-end sensor suites, which inherently limit image diversity and scalability. While in-the-wild images are abundant, their noisy geo-tags make them unsuitable for reliable evaluation. To bridge this gap, we introduce OpenCVL, a large-scale, diverse, and open dataset containing 617,388 ground-aerial image pairs spanning 41 cities across four European countries. All images are sourced from permissive platforms, ensuring long-term accessibility and supporting open and reproducible research. The training set combines images captured with high-end sensors with diverse in-the-wild imagery. We further develop a data curation framework that filters and corrects pose annotations to construct reliable in-the-wild evaluation data. In addition, OpenCVL includes dedicated cross-area and snowy test sets to assess generalization and robustness. Experiments with a state-of-the-art CVL model on OpenCVL show that incorporating noisy in-the-wild data consistently improves performance on clean test sets, suggesting a promising direction for scaling CVL with diverse real-world imagery.

</details>


### [14] [PointRL: Learning Point-Level Vision-Language Grounding from Verifiable Annotation Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25299)
*Jingyang Su,Pu Cao,Xiuze Jin,Longyue Zhang,Qing Song,Lu Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PointRL，一种可验证的强化学习框架，用于学习视觉语言模型（VLM）的逐点级视觉定位（point-level grounding）。PointRL将已有的异质标注（边界框、掩码、实例标签）转换为指向指令，同时保留标注作为隐藏的验证证据，由确定性检查器对预测进行评分。奖励函数评估可解析性、点有效性、实例覆盖、基数一致性和冗余/缺失预测。在PointArena上将Qwen3.5-4B准确率从56.11%提升至65.58%，并在RoboSpatial、BLINK和Ref-Adv等外部基准上取得同骨干网络的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLM越来越多地依赖点坐标作为GUI交互、机器人操作和交互式视觉系统中的紧凑可执行接口，但学习可靠的指向行为面临困难，因为监督空间本质上不唯一：同一目标区域内可能有多个有效坐标，而多实例指令还要求目标覆盖、计数一致性和重复抑制。

Method: PointRL将边界框、掩码和实例标签转换为指向指令，但保留其目标支持、实例成员关系和集合约束作为提示之外的隐藏验证证据。使用确定性检查器对预测进行评分，奖励函数涵盖可解析性、点有效性、实例覆盖、基数一致性以及冗余或缺失预测五个方面。

Result: 在PointArena上，PointRL将Qwen3.5-4B的整体准确率从56.11%提升至65.58%。在RoboSpatial、BLINK和Ref-Adv等外部基准上的评估显示，在相同骨干网络下均取得增益，表明可验证的点级反馈有助于空间定位。

Conclusion: 可验证的点级反馈信号（PointRL框架）能够有效提升VLM的逐点视觉定位能力，不仅在本领域内表现出显著提升，还能泛化到多个外部空间定位基准上，说明基于确定性验证的强化学习方法在空间接地任务中具有广泛适用潜力。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) increasingly rely on point coordinates as a compact and executable interface for visual grounding in GUI interaction, robotic manipulation, and interactive visual systems. However, learning reliable pointing behavior remains difficult because the supervision space is inherently non-unique: many coordinates may be valid within the same target region, while multi-instance instructions require target coverage, count consistency, and duplicate suppression. This work presents PointRL, a verifiable reinforcement learning framework that learns point-level grounding from existing heterogeneous annotation evidence. PointRL converts bounding boxes, masks, and instance labels into pointing instructions, while retaining their target supports, instance membership, and set constraints as hidden verifier evidence, i.e., annotations kept outside the prompt and used by a deterministic checker to score predictions. The proposed reward evaluates parseability, point validity, instance coverage, cardinality consistency, and redundant or missing predictions. On PointArena, PointRL improves the overall accuracy of Qwen3.5-4B from 56.11% to 65.58%. Further evaluations on RoboSpatial, BLINK, and Ref-Adv show same-backbone gains on the evaluated external benchmarks, suggesting that verifiable point-level feedback may benefit spatial grounding in these settings.

</details>


### [15] [WAVE: Reversing the Guidance Hierarchy for Coarse-to-Fine Guided Depth Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25302)
*Tayyab Nasir,Daochang Liu,Ajmal Mian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WAVE提出了一种基于多级离散小波变换（ML-DWT）的显式特征控制机制，通过逆序消费子带和语义token实现由粗到细的深度超分辨率重建，并在多个基准上匹配或超越现有方法，尤其在高上采样倍数下表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GDSR方法通过卷积层级提取RGB引导特征，天然存在"由细到粗"的偏差：低级空间线索出现在浅层，深层网络会抑制与真实深度边界不对应的线索，导致伪影和边缘模糊。这种偏差在基于语义的方法中也存在，因为它们早期消费低层token、晚期才消费全局token。

Method: WAVE引入多级离散小波变换（ML-DWT）作为显式且可解释的特征控制机制，按子带和语义token生成顺序的逆序进行粗到细重建。利用子带分别处理高、低频内容，在源头过滤误导性的RGB颜色和纹理线索。将结构和细节重建分离为专用模块：1）建模小波子带、深度特征和语义先验的内部及跨域交互；2）对高频带应用语义门控；3）通过可逆耦合机制融合模态以防止单模态坍缩。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，WAVE匹配或超越了现有方法；在高上采样倍数下（低分辨率深度包含最少结构信息时）取得最大增益。

Conclusion: WAVE通过显式的小波域特征控制和粗到细的重建策略，提供了一种直观且可解释的替代方案，有效解决了传统GDSR方法中隐式抑制误导性线索导致的边缘模糊和伪影问题。

Abstract: Guided depth super-resolution (GDSR) typically extracts RGB guidance features through convolutional hierarchies, inheriting their fine-to-coarse bias. Thus, low-level spatial cues surface in early layers, leaving the deeper layers to suppress those that do not correspond to true depth boundaries, which risks artifacts and blurred edges. The same fine-to-coarse bias persists in semantics-based methods that consume low-level tokens early and global tokens late. We present WAVE, which introduces a multi-level discrete wavelet transform (ML-DWT) as an explicit and interpretable feature-control mechanism, enabling a coarse-to-fine reconstruction by consuming sub-bands and semantic tokens in reverse of their generation order. WAVE further exploits these sub-bands to treat high- and low-frequency content separately, filtering at its source the misleading RGB color and texture cues that often lead to blurred boundaries and artifacts, offering an intuitive alternative to the suppression learned implicitly by an opaque network. WAVE separates structure and detail reconstruction into dedicated modules that: i) model interactions within and across wavelet sub-bands, depth features, and semantic priors, ii) apply semantic gating to the high-frequency bands, and iii) fuse modalities through an invertible coupling mechanism that prevents collapse onto a single modality. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that WAVE matches or outperforms existing methods, with the largest gains at high upsampling factors, where low-resolution depth contains the least structure.

</details>


### [16] [MulVec: Fine-Grained Role-Aware Matching for Training-Free Zero-Shot Composed Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25305)
*Zihao Zhang,Dayan Wu,Xinze Liu,Hengjie Zhu,Yiliang Zhu,Ding Wang,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin,Weiping Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MULVEC，一种无需训练的组合图像检索方法，通过将查询结构化为"全局描述、期望、保留、禁止"四种检索角色，结合全局与局部视觉向量进行角色感知匹配，显著提升零样本组合图像检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本组合图像检索方法通常将目标整体描述并与全局图像表示匹配，这种方式会混淆不同语义线索且丢失细粒度细节。

Method: MULVEC通过编译器生成结构化查询记录，映射为四种检索角色（全局描述/期望/保留/禁止）；使用冻结编码器将查询映射为一个目标描述向量和多个角色专用探测向量，每个候选用一个全局视觉向量和局部视觉向量库表示；检索角色共享证据分别打分，最后通过固定加权求和一次性排名整个图库。

Result: 在CIRCO、CIRR和FashionIQ三个基准及三种骨干网络上验证，MULVEC在CIRCO mAP@5上相比最强对比方法提升高达23.0%，并在CIRR和FashionIQ上取得最佳结果。

Conclusion: 角色感知的查询结构化方法能有效利用全局与局部视觉证据，在不进行任务特定训练的情况下大幅提升零样本组合图像检索的精度。

Abstract: Training-free zero-shot composed image retrieval finds a target image in a gallery from a reference image and a text edit without learning from task-specific image triplets. Existing methods typically describe the target as a whole and match this description with a global image representation. This global matching can mix different semantic cues and lose fine- grained details. We propose MULVEC, a role-aware method whose compiler produces a structured query record that is mapped to four retrieval roles: Global describes the full target, Desired states what should appear, Preserve states what should remain, and Forbidden states what should disappear. Frozen encoders map the query to one target description vector and role-specific probe vectors, while each candidate is represented by one global visual vector and a bank of local visual vectors. The retrieval roles then use this shared evidence for their respective purposes, and a fixed weighted sum of their scores ranks the entire gallery in a single retrieval pass. Across CIRCO, CIRR, and FashionIQ and three backbone scales, MULVEC improves CIRCO mAP@5 by up to 23.0% over the strongest compared method and gives the best CIRR and FashionIQ results in our comparison.

</details>


### [17] [V-Link: Recovering Lost Visual Representations in Action DiT for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25308)
*Yehao Lu,Jiarui Yang,Yuning Su,Yufeng Xie,Yu Zhong,Yazhou Zhang,Haiyu Lan,Kaixiang Lu,Peiwen Lin,Chuang Wang,Zequn Qin,Enyu Li,Xi Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出V-Link方法，通过在学习视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中显式恢复视觉表征，解决动作专家对VLM特征中3D几何和2D语义信息访问受限的问题，在多个仿真和真实机器人任务上显著提升操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽然为通用机器人操作提供了可扩展路径，但现有VLA架构存在关键缺陷：动作专家对VLM特征中的3D几何和2D语义信息访问有限，这种可访问性差距削弱了感知锚定能力，限制了精细机器人操作的性能。

Method: V-Link在视觉-语言到动作的特征传递过程中显式恢复视觉表征。具体而言，V-Link在VLM内部学习互补的空间查询（Spatial Queries）和语义查询（Semantic Queries）表示，并通过不对称路径注入到动作DiT中。语义查询补充原始VLM图像token，而空间查询为空间锚定的动作生成提供专用的几何条件。

Result: 在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus和RoboTwin 2.0基准上，V-Link相较于基础模型GR00T N1.6的平均成功率分别提升了+1.9%、+31.2%和+18.8%。在AGIBOT A3 Ultra上的两个真实世界人形任务中，V-Link分别获得了+20%和+24%的性能提升。

Conclusion: V-Link通过显式恢复VLM到动作传递过程中的视觉表征，有效弥合了动作专家对3D几何和2D语义信息访问的差距，在多个仿真环境和真实机器人任务上均验证了其方法有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models provide a scalable path toward generalist robotic manipulation by integrating visual perception, language understanding, and continuous action control. However, we reveal a critical limitation of VLA architectures: the action expert has limited access to the 3D geometric and 2D semantic information available in VLM features. This accessibility gap weakens perceptual grounding and limits performance on fine-grained robotic manipulation. To address this issue, we propose V-Link, which explicitly recovers visual representations during the vision-language (VL) to action (A) feature transfer. Specifically, V-Link learns complementary Spatial and Semantic Query representations within the VLM and injects them into Action DiT through asymmetric pathways. Semantic Queries complement the original VLM image tokens, whereas Spatial Queries provide dedicated geometric conditioning for spatially grounded action generation. Across LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus, and RoboTwin 2.0, our V-Link improves the average success rate over base model GR00T N1.6 by +1.9%, +31.2%, and +18.8%, respectively. On the AGIBOT A3 Ultra, V-Link further achieves gains of +20% and +24% on two real-world humanoid tasks.

</details>


### [18] [Not All Attention Heads Contribute to Critical Visual Token Selection: Head-Aware Pruning Matters More](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25332)
*Chaofang Ma,Lin Jiang,Carol Jingyi Li,Xingyu Liu,Zeyu Li,Jiang Xu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ProViP，一种无需训练的渐进式视觉标记剪枝框架，通过利用注意力头子集的关键视觉标记定位能力，在保持95.9%原始性能的同时实现1.62倍推理加速和88.9%的剪枝率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）的成功依赖于大量视觉标记，导致推理时显存和计算开销巨大、延迟增加。现有视觉标记剪枝方法通过聚合所有注意力头的分数来评估标记重要性，但本文发现关键视觉标记的定位能力仅集中在少数注意力头上，聚合所有头反而会降低性能。

Method: 提出ProViP框架，包含两个阶段：(1) 在LLM骨干推理前，基于输入标记的嵌入相似度移除冗余视觉标记；(2) 在推理过程中，采用头感知（head-aware）剪枝策略，仅聚合少数关键注意力头的分数来剪枝视觉标记。

Result: 在LLaVA-1.5-7B上，ProViP在88.9%的剪枝率下保留了95.9%的原始性能，并实现了1.62倍的推理加速，在任务性能和推理效率上均表现优异。

Conclusion: ProViP证明头感知的视觉标记剪枝策略优于传统的全头注意力聚合方法，能够在不需训练的情况下显著提升VLM推理效率，同时保持高质量的任务表现。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have exhibited impressive performance across diverse visual scenarios. However, this success comes at the cost of explosive growth in visual tokens, which imposes substantial memory and computational overhead during inference, ultimately increasing latency. To improve VLM inference efficiency, a typical class of visual token pruning methods estimates token importance by aggregating attention scores across all heads in the pruning layer of the Large Language Model (LLM) backbone and prunes tokens based on aggregated scores. However, in this paper, we reveal a compelling phenomenon: the capability to pinpoint critical visual tokens is concentrated within a small fraction of heads. Aggregation exclusively on these heads can improve task performance. Inspired by this observation, we propose ProViP, a training-free progressive visual token pruning framework. ProViP first removes redundant visual tokens based on the embedding similarity of input tokens before reasoning of the LLM backbone, and then further prunes tokens during reasoning via head-aware pruning. Experiments demonstrate that ProViP delivers outstanding task performance and inference efficiency. For instance, when applied to LLaVA-1.5-7B, ProViP retains 95.9% of the original performance and achieves 1.62x inference speedup under an 88.9% pruning ratio.

</details>


### [19] [GraftSR: Grafting Authentic Textures for Real-World Image Super-Resolution via Identical-Instance Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25334)
*Qifan Yu,Haoran Bai,Zongyao He,Weijie He,Sibin Deng,Honggang Qi,Ying Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GraftSR，一种基于纹理参考引导的生成式超分辨率框架，通过双掩码参考引导机制解决真实图像超分辨率中的纹理幻觉问题，并构建了首个大规模数据集TexRefSR-141K及基准TexRefSR-Eval，在LPIPS指标上较最优基线降低20.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的真实图像超分辨率虽然感知质量优异，但存在严重的纹理幻觉问题，即生成的纹理并非真实存在而是模型臆造的。现有方法缺乏利用同实例参考图锚定真实纹理恢复的能力。

Method: GraftSR采用双掩码参考引导机制，系统解耦跨视角纹理注入过程：一方面明确从参考图中提取哪些真实纹理，另一方面精确定位在目标图像中何处应用这些纹理，从而在不依赖脆弱的空间对齐的前提下实现鲁棒纹理迁移。同时构建了TexRefSR-141K数据集，提供带互补空间掩码的高质量参考元组。

Result: 在新建立的基准TexRefSR-Eval上，GraftSR刷新了最先进水平，LPIPS指标较最优基线降低20.2%，实现了更贴近参考图像的真实感修复效果。

Conclusion: GraftSR通过解耦纹理提取与定位过程，有效解决了参考图像与低质量输入之间空间错位带来的挑战，验证了参考引导方法在真实图像超分辨率中的有效性，同时提供的数据集和基准为该领域后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Diffusion-based real-world image super-resolution (SR) achieves impressive perceptual quality but inherently suffers from severe texture hallucination. To overcome this limitation, we propose GraftSR, a texture-reference-guided generative SR framework that leverages reference images of the identical instance to anchor the restoration of authentic textures. However, severe spatial misalignment between low-quality inputs and their references poses significant challenges, often leading to ambiguous transfer targets and background feature leakage. To address these issues, GraftSR employs a novel dual-mask reference guidance mechanism that systematically decouples the cross-view texture injection process. By explicitly isolating what authentic textures to extract from the reference and precisely localizing where to apply them within the target, GraftSR achieves robust texture transfer without relying on brittle spatial alignment. Furthermore, to bridge the critical gap in appropriate training data, we construct TexRefSR-141K, the first large-scale dataset providing high-quality reference tuples equipped with complementary spatial masks. Extensive experiments on our newly established benchmark, TexRefSR-Eval, demonstrate that GraftSR sets a new state-of-the-art. Notably, it reduces LPIPS by 20.2\% over top-performing baselines, achieving superior reference-faithful restoration.

</details>


### [20] [CoRE: Weakly Supervised Coarse-to-Fine Risk Evidence Learning in Driving Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25344)
*Kaiser Hamid,Can Cui,Nade Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoRE框架，一种弱监督的从粗到细学习方法，仅利用视频级别的粗标签即可学习时间与实体级别的细粒度风险支持预测，无需昂贵的细粒度标注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驾驶中的感知风险随时间演变且可能由特定场景实体支持，但现有监督通常仅限于粗粒度的视频级别判断。学习支撑证据何时出现以及哪些实体支撑风险预测通常需要昂贵的时间级和实体级标注。

Method: CoRE首先训练一个视频级预测器并冻结它，然后通过结构化干预候选时间区域或实体轨迹来测量每个候选如何改变粗粒度预测，生成分级预测效应目标。这些目标被蒸馏到一个学生模型中，使其能够直接从原始视频预测时间和实体支持，推理时无需干预。

Result: 在三个互补设置中评估：RISEE测试主观片段级判断的感知风险支持；DoTA提供独立时间事件标注用于评估弱监督交通异常定位；UCF-Crime测试该机制能否扩展到非驾驶异常检测基准。CoRE在DoTA上展现出强时间定位性能，在UCF-Crime上表现具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 粗粒度视频预测可以为恢复支撑它们的细粒度证据提供有效的监督信号，无需相应的细粒度标签，证明了粗到细学习原则的泛化能力。

Abstract: Perceived risk in driving evolves over time and may be supported by specific scene entities, yet supervision is typically limited to coarse video-level judgments. Learning \emph{when} supporting evidence emerges and \emph{which entities} support a risk predictor would ordinarily require costly temporal- and entity-level annotations. We introduce \textbf{CoRE}, a weakly supervised coarse-to-fine framework that learns fine-grained prediction support from coarse video supervision. CoRE first trains a video-level predictor and then freezes it. Structured interventions over candidate temporal regions or entity tracks measure how each candidate changes the coarse prediction, producing graded prediction-effect targets. These targets are distilled into a student that directly predicts temporal and entity support from the original video, without requiring interventions at inference. We evaluate this learning principle across three complementary settings: RISEE tests perceived-risk support from subjective clip-level judgments without temporal or entity-level risk annotations; DoTA provides independent temporal event annotations for evaluating weakly supervised traffic-anomaly localization; and UCF-Crime tests whether the same coarse-to-fine mechanism extends to a standard non-driving anomaly-detection benchmark. Across these settings, CoRE learns informative fine-grained support from coarse supervision, with strong temporal localization on DoTA and competitive performance on UCF-Crime. These results show that coarse video predictions can provide useful supervision for recovering the fine-grained evidence supporting them, without requiring corresponding fine-grained labels.

</details>


### [21] [Where to Look Matters: On-Policy Self-Distillation for Long-Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25356)
*Kaishen Wang,Dongdi Zhao,Yijun Liang,Dingqiang Ye,Ruibo Chen,Heng Huang,Di Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Clue-OPSD框架，利用问题相关线索片段作为特权监督信号，通过在线策略自蒸馏训练全视频学生模型，在不依赖答案标签和推理时额外模块的情况下提升长视频理解性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频中全视频上下文包含大量与问题无关的时间内容，会分散模型注意力，降低特定问题回答的准确性。作者发现聚焦于包含问题相关证据的短线索片段比使用全视频更能提升预测精度。

Method: 提出了Clue-OPSD（clue-privileged on-policy self-distillation）框架：训练时，全视频学生模型从以线索区间为条件的自教师模型中学习，通过对齐学生生成的轨迹上的下一token分布来实现蒸馏。线索区间作为特权监督信号，不依赖真实答案标签。

Result: 在多个长视频理解基准和Qwen3.5不同模型规模上，Clue-OPSD相比对应骨干模型和受监督的后训练基线均展现一致性的性能提升。

Conclusion: 利用线索区间作为特权监督的自蒸馏方法能有效提升长视频理解能力，训练时无需答案标签，推理时无需线索标注和额外模块，具备实用性和泛化性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have made substantial progress in long-video understanding, with standard backbone models typically answering questions from frames sampled across the full video. However, as videos become longer, the full-video context inevitably contains more question-irrelevant temporal content, which can distract the model from the evidence needed to answer a specific question. We empirically find that focusing the visual input on short annotated clue intervals containing question-relevant evidence consistently improves prediction accuracy across model scales compared with using the corresponding full videos, while requiring fewer input frames. Based on this finding, we introduce Clue-OPSD, a clue-privileged on-policy self-distillation framework for long-video understanding. During training, a full-video student learns from a self-teacher conditioned on the corresponding clue interval by aligning their next-token distributions along student-generated trajectories. Clue-OPSD thus uses clue intervals as privileged supervision without relying on ground-truth answer labels, while requiring no clue annotations or additional modules at inference time. Extensive experiments across multiple long-video understanding benchmarks and Qwen3.5 model scales demonstrate consistent improvements over the corresponding backbone models and strong performance against supervised post-training baselines.

</details>


### [22] [FlashNormal: Detailed Surface Normal Estimation from Flash and No-Flash Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25360)
*Ruiyang Chen,Feiran Li,Heng Guo,Zhanyu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FlashNormal，一种基于扩散模型的闪光/无闪光图像对表面法线估计方法，利用闪光引起的阴影变化和曲率引导的细节增强策略，提高表面细节恢复并缓解形状-反射率歧义。同时提出EvalFlash数据集（含20个真实物体及对应法线真值）用于定量评估，实验证明其优于现有单图像方法及闪光/无闪光方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图像法线估计方法难以恢复精细表面细节且易受形状-反射率歧义影响；光度立体视觉虽精度高但需要多光源采集设置，实用性受限。因此需要一种既保留智能手机高实用性又能利用光照变化提升法线估计精度的方法。

Method: 提出FlashNormal，一种基于扩散模型的闪光/无闪光图像对表面法线估计方法。利用闪光照明的阴影变化信息，结合曲率引导的细节增强策略，改善表面细节恢复并缓解形状-反射率歧义问题。同时构建了EvalFlash数据集，包含20个真实物体及其对齐的GT表面法线用于定量基准测试。

Result: 大量实验表明FlashNormal在表面法线估计上优于最先进的单图像方法，并且在EvalFlash基准上显著超越现有的闪光/无闪光法线估计方法。

Conclusion: 通过结合闪光/无闪光图像对的实用性与扩散模型及曲率引导增强策略，FlashNormal有效解决了单图像法线估计的细节丢失和歧义问题，为智能手机等实际场景下的高保真表面法线估计提供了新方案。

Abstract: High-quality surface normal estimation is preferred for detailed surface shape recovery and image editing. Existing single image-based methods, though being a practical setup, often struggle to recover fine surface details and are sensitive to inherent shape-reflectance ambiguity. While photometric stereo achieves high-fidelity surface normal estimation from images under varying lights, its applicability is strictly limited by requiring a multi-illumination capture setup. To this end, we propose FlashNormal, a diffusion-based surface normal estimator from flash/no-flash image pairs. While retaining high practicability on modern smartphones, our proposal takes advantage of flash-induced shading variations, and leverages curvature-guided detail enhancement strategy, improving surface detail recovery and mitigating shape-reflectance ambiguity effectively. To evaluate our proposed method, we further present EvalFlash, the first real-world flash/no-flash evaluation dataset containing 20 objects aligned with ground-truth surface normals for quantitative benchmarking. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of FlashNormal over state-of-the-art single image-based methods and show a significant out-performance over flash/no-flash-based normal estimation method on EvalFlash.

</details>


### [23] [RSFusionDet: Underwater RGB-Sonar Multimodal Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25367)
*Zhuoyan Liu,Yihan Wang,Bo Wang,Bing Wang,Ye Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文创建了RGB-Sonar多模态目标检测数据集RSFusion并提出了相应的评估指标，同时设计了RSFusionDet检测器，通过Cross-Attention Fusion模块融合RGB和声纳图像的互补信息，并利用Object Matching Head和Loss实现跨模态目标匹配，在RSFusion数据集上取得了优于其他模型的检测和匹配性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下单模态目标检测面临诸多挑战：光学图像受水下噪声和可见距离限制，声纳图像缺少目标结构信息。而RGB图像具有丰富的结构信息，声纳图像受噪声影响小且可见距离远，两者具有互补性，因此需要探索RGB-Sonar多模态融合检测方法。

Method: 1) 创建RGB-Sonar多模态目标检测数据集RSFusion并提出基准评估指标；2) 提出RSFusionDet检测器；3) 设计Cross-Attention Fusion (CAFusion)模块用于融合RGB-Sonar空间错位特征；4) 设计Object Matching Head (OMHead)与OMLoss实现跨模态相同目标匹配。

Result: RSFusionDet在RSFusion数据集上达到76.4/48.6 AP（RGB/Sonar）的目标检测性能和83.4 F1-Score_match的目标匹配性能，优于其他目标检测模型。相比DINO基线，RGB/Sonar AP分别提升0.7/1.4，同时提供可靠的跨模态目标匹配。

Conclusion: RGB-Sonar多模态融合方法能有效利用两种模态的互补信息，不仅提升水下目标检测精度，还实现了可靠的跨模态目标匹配，为解决水下目标检测难题提供了新的思路和公开资源。

Abstract: Underwater unimodal object detection faces many challenges in sensor imaging, such as optical images limited by underwater noise and visible distance, and sonar images limited by less object structural information. While, optical images have rich object structural information, and sonar images are less affected by underwater noise and have a longer visible distance. Optical (RGB modality) and sonar (Sonar modality) images have complementary information underwater. In this paper, we create an RGB-Sonar multimodal object detection dataset, \textbf{R}GB-\textbf{S}onar \textbf{Fusion} (RSFusion) and propose evaluation metrics for the benchmark. And we propose the \textbf{R}GB-\textbf{S}onar \textbf{Fusion} \textbf{Det}ector (RSFusionDet) with a new RGB-Sonar multimodal object detection result expression for RGB-Sonar multimodal object detection. We analyze the features of RGB and Sonar modal information, and design a Cross-Attention Fusion (CAFusion) module to fuse RGB-Sonar spatial misalignment features and Object Matching Head (OMHead) with Loss (OMLoss) to match identical objects in RGB-Sonar modalities. Our RSFusionDet achieves 76.4/48.6 AP (RGB/Sonar) for object detection and 83.4 \(\text{F1-Score}_{match}\) for object matching, on RSFusion, which outperforms other object detection models. Compared with the DINO baseline, our method improves by 0.7/1.4 AP (RGB/Sonar) while simultaneously providing reliable cross-modal object matching. The code and datasets are publicly available at https://github.com/LEFTeyex/RSFusionDet.

</details>


### [24] [Capacity Overflow: A Blind Spot for Backdoor Attacks in Vision MoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25371)
*Xiaocheng Zou,Tiancheng Zheng,Xiaolin Xu,Ruyi Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用视觉Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）架构中容量受限的令牌调度机制（其依赖批处理大小）的隐蔽供应链后门攻击。攻击通过三阶段框架实现：在早期MoE层注入后门，在深层训练中和器以在正常容量下抑制后门，并通过批处理自适应容量因子使大批量部署时中和器因令牌溢出而失效。该攻击在小批量安全审计时保持休眠（ASR低于9%），在大批量部署时激活（ASR 76-87%），并能规避多种主流防御方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉MoE架构的容量受限令牌调度机制依赖推理批处理大小，这一批处理相关的行为特性未被视为攻击面。作者旨在揭示这一安全隐患，展示如何利用MoE固有的执行特性构建能够在审计时保持休眠、部署时激活的隐蔽后门攻击。

Method: 三阶段攻击框架：（1）在早期MoE层注入后门触发器；（2）在更深MoE层训练一个中和器，使其在正常容量条件下抑制后门效应；（3）配置批处理自适应容量因子——对小批量保持高容量、对大批量降低容量，使大批量部署时令牌超出容量限制导致中和器被绕过，从而自然激活后门。

Result: 在V-MoE和Swin-MoE架构上，基于ImageNet-100和GTSRB数据集，激活模式下攻击成功率达76-87%，休眠模式ASR低于9%。该攻击能够规避Neural Cleanse、STRIP、Fine-Pruning和Activation Clustering等四种主流后门防御方法。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了一个由可扩展视觉MoE架构中批处理依赖执行特性引发的根本性安全风险，表明即使经过常规安全审计的模型仍可能在部署阶段遭受隐蔽攻击，强调了在MoE架构安全设计中考虑批处理动态特性的必要性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) has become a prevalent paradigm for scaling Vision Transformers efficiently. To ensure computational scalability and prevent expert overload, Vision MoE architectures employ a capacity-bounded token dispatch mechanism, where each expert's processing budget depends on the inference batch size. This work identifies this batch-dependent behavior as an overlooked attack surface, and proposes a stealthy supply-chain backdoor attack that exploits this property through a three-phase framework. First, we inject a backdoor into an early MoE layer. Second, we train a neutralizer in a deeper MoE layer that suppresses the backdoor under normal capacity. Third, we configure a batch-adaptive capacity factor that preserves high capacity for small batches while reducing it for large batches, naturally disabling the neutralizer via token overflow at deployment-scale batch sizes. The attack remains in dormant mode during small-batch security audits and enters activation mode during large-batch deployment. Experiments on V-MoE and Swin-MoE across ImageNet-100 and GTSRB demonstrate activation-mode attack success rates of 76-87% with dormant-mode ASR below 9%, while evading Neural Cleanse, STRIP, Fine-Pruning, and Activation Clustering. Our findings reveal a fundamental security risk arising from batch-dependent execution in scalable Vision MoE architectures.

</details>


### [25] [Efficient Training with Foresight: Multi-Token Auxiliary Supervision for Autoregressive Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25386)
*Guo Niu,Xiongfei Yao,Teng Wang,Nannan Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MTAR（多令牌自回归）统一训练框架，通过多令牌预测（MTP）、令牌级对比正则化（TCR）和语义丢弃（SD）三个组件，分别改善预测目标稀疏性、表示判别性和训练效率。在ImageNet上相比LlamaGen，FID最多降低0.95，训练速度提升39%，且仅用1/3训练迭代即可达到或超过基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的自回归图像生成中的下一个令牌预测（NTP）存在三个问题：监督信号稀疏且目光短浅、表示判别性不足、以及全序列密集计算导致训练成本高。作者旨在解决这些制约自回归图像生成效率和质量的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出MTAR框架，包含三个训练阶段组件：1）多令牌预测（MTP）对多个未来令牌施加联合监督以缓解NTP的稀疏性和短视性；2）令牌级对比正则化（TCR）显式增强采样令牌表示的可分离性；3）语义丢弃（SD）作为语义感知的训练加速策略，减少低信息令牌上的冗余计算。三个组件仅在训练阶段启用，推理时零额外开销。

Result: 在ImageNet数据集上，MTAR相比LlamaGen基线实现了更好的生成质量与训练效率平衡，FID最多降低0.95，训练速度提升39%；即使仅使用1/3的训练迭代，仍能达到与基线相当或更优的性能。

Conclusion: MTAR通过同时改进预测目标、表示正则化和训练效率三方面，有效解决了传统自回归图像生成的关键局限，在不增加推理开销的前提下显著提升生成质量和训练效率，证明了多令牌预测策略在自回归图像生成中的有效性。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) image generation has shown strong potential for scalable high-fidelity synthesis by modeling images as discrete token sequences. However, traditional next token prediction (NTP) continues to suffer from sparse and myopic supervision, insufficiently discriminative representations, and high training cost caused by dense computation over the full token sequence. To address these issues, we propose multi-token autoregressive (MTAR), a unified training framework that improves autoregressive image generation from three aspects: prediction objectives, representation regularization, and training efficiency. Specifically, MTAR introduces multi-token prediction (MTP) to alleviate the sparsity and myopia of traditional NTP by imposing joint supervision on multiple future tokens; employs token-level contrastive regularization (TCR) to explicitly enhance the separability of sampled token representations and thereby improve representation discriminability; and incorporates semantic dropping (SD) as a semantics-aware training acceleration strategy to reduce redundant computation on low-information tokens while preserving informative learning signals. All three components are applied only during training and introduce no additional overhead during autoregressive inference. On ImageNet, MTAR achieves a better balance between generation quality and training efficiency. Compared with LlamaGen, MTAR achieves up to 0.95 lower FID and 39\% faster training. Moreover, even with only 1/3 of the training iterations, it still attains performance comparable to or better than the baseline, substantially reducing training time.

</details>


### [26] [PIVOT: A Multi-Trajectory Dataset and Testbed for Pose, Intrinsics, and Novel Viewpoint Evaluation in Real-World 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25401)
*Mary Raymond*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PIVOT，一个面向机器人和自主系统场景的多轨迹数据集、处理管道和评估框架，用于独立研究NeRF和3DGS等新视角合成方法在真实世界条件下的性能，包括未见过轨迹的泛化能力、测量位姿与优化位姿的敏感性以及标定与优化内参的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的NeRF、3DGS等新视角合成方法评估通常使用过于干净的采集和重建条件，包括重建友好的轨迹、优化过的相机位姿和内参，以及从训练轨迹中采样的测试视图。这些假设掩盖了在机器人、无人机和自主系统中遇到的真实问题：测量位姿存在噪声、相机标定需要可复用、以及相机路径与训练轨迹结构不同。

Method: PIVOT采用多轨迹数据采集方式，每场景使用多种相机轨迹进行拍摄，同时保留传感器测量位姿和COLMAP优化位姿，以及标定和优化的内参。定义了三个基准家族：(1) 训练轨迹上未见视图与未见过轨迹的泛化比较；(2) 测量位姿与优化位姿的敏感性；(3) 标定内参与优化内参的敏感性。此外提出有向位姿空间Chamfer距离来量化训练位姿对评估轨迹的覆盖程度。数据集包含5个真实场景，由DJI Mini 4 Pro采集，提供基于Nerfstudio的开源评估工具链。

Result: 基准测试结果显示，已表示轨迹上的未见视图与未见过轨迹之间存在一致的性能差距，同时对位姿来源和相机内参表现出显著的敏感性。

Conclusion: PIVOT揭示了现有评估方法在理想化条件下进行导致对真实世界部署性能的过度估计。该框架为独立研究轨迹泛化、位姿和内参敏感性提供了标准化测试平台，有助于推动新视角合成方法在机器人等现实场景中的鲁棒性研究。

Abstract: Neural radiance fields (NeRFs), 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), and related novel-view synthesis methods are commonly evaluated under capture and reconstruction conditions cleaner than those encountered by robots, drones, and autonomous systems. Benchmarks often rely on reconstruction-friendly trajectories, optimized camera poses and intrinsics, and held-out views sampled from trajectories represented during training. These assumptions can obscure performance with measured poses, reusable camera calibration, and structurally different camera paths.
  We introduce PIVOT (Pose, Intrinsics and Viewpoint Oriented Testbed), a multi-trajectory dataset, processing pipeline, and evaluation framework for independently studying these factors. PIVOT captures each scene using diverse camera trajectories and retains, where available, both sensor-derived measured poses and COLMAP-optimized poses, together with calibrated and optimized camera intrinsics. It defines three benchmark families: (1) seen versus unseen trajectory novel-view generalization, (2) measured versus optimized pose sensitivity, and (3) calibrated versus optimized intrinsics sensitivity. We also introduce a directed pose-space Chamfer distance to quantify how well training poses cover an evaluation trajectory.
  PIVOT v1 contains five real-world scenes captured with a DJI Mini 4 Pro and provides an open processing and Nerfstudio-based evaluation toolchain. Benchmark results show a consistent quality gap between held-out views on represented trajectories and unseen trajectories, as well as substantial sensitivity to pose source and camera intrinsics.

</details>


### [27] [AdaptiveEmbed: Sample-Adaptive Multi-Vector Representation for Multimodal Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25412)
*Xinze Liu,Lei Yang,Dayan Wu,Hengjie Zhu,Zihao Zhang,Hanqi Wu,Tianzhu Hu,Peng Fu,Zheng Lin,Weiping Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出样本自适应多向量表示（SAMVR），一种新的多模态检索问题设定，根据每个样本的检索需求动态分配表示向量数量，并通过AdaptiveEmbed框架（包含多组对比学习MGCL和边际效用分配MUA）实现容量自适应分配，实验证明其优于固定容量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多向量表示方法对所有样本使用固定数量的向量，忽略不同样本对表示容量的差异化需求，导致检索性能次优。

Method: 提出SAMVR问题设定，每个样本使用内容自适应嵌入集（CAES）表示；通过AdaptiveEmbed框架实现，包括多组对比学习（MGCL）学习结构化多向量表示，以及效用策略优化（UPO）中的边际效用分配（MUA）确定样本特定容量。

Result: 在图像、文本、视频和音频的多模态检索基准上，样本自适应容量分配整体优于固定容量多向量表示，验证了SAMVR的有效性。

Conclusion: SAMVR是多向量多模态检索中自适应容量分配的有效框架，为多模态检索提供了新的可行方向。

Abstract: Multi-vector representations have emerged as an effective paradigm for multimodal retrieval, representing each sample with multiple complementary embeddings to capture fine-grained cross-modal information. However, existing approaches typically employ a fixed representation capacity, assigning the same number of vectors to all samples regardless of their individual retrieval demands. Such a fixed-capacity formulation overlooks the fact that different samples may require different amounts of representation capacity for effective retrieval. In this work, we introduce \emph{Sample-Adaptive Multi-Vector Representation} (SAMVR), a new problem setting for multimodal retrieval that studies how multi-vector representation capacity can be allocated at the sample level. Under SAMVR, each sample is represented by a \emph{content-adaptive embedding set} (CAES), whose capacity is determined according to the sample-specific retrieval utility of additional representation vectors. To instantiate SAMVR, we propose \emph{AdaptiveEmbed}, a unified framework for learning sample-adaptive multi-vector representations. AdaptiveEmbed learns structured multi-vector representations through \emph{Multi-Group Contrastive Learning} (MGCL) with the symmetric \emph{set-to-set similarity} (SetSim), and further employs \emph{Utility Policy Optimization} (UPO) to determine sample-specific representation capacity via \emph{Marginal Utility Allocation} (MUA). Experiments across multimodal retrieval benchmarks involving image, text, video, and audio show that sample-adaptive capacity allocation achieves overall better retrieval performance than fixed-capacity multi-vector representations, validating the effectiveness of SAMVR for multimodal retrieval. These results establish SAMVR as a viable formulation for adaptive capacity allocation in multi-vector multimodal retrieval.

</details>


### [28] [Bootstrapping a 4D LiDAR Annotation Tool from Video Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25418)
*Jihun Kim,Hyun-Kurl Jang,Hyemin Yang,Jinnyeong Yang,Hyeokjun Kweon,Kuk-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LiDAR-SAM2是一个将2D视频基础模型SAM2转化为4D LiDAR监督来源的框架，无需人工标注即可自动生成时间上一致的LiDAR级标签，训练出的模型在SemanticKITTI上接近全人工标注的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D LiDAR分割的研究受限于数据标注的高成本和难以扩展的问题。手动为稀疏点云序列分配时间一致的标签非常昂贵，且每个新任务或新领域都需要重新标注，因此迫切需要一种无需人工标注即可自动生成高质量LiDAR训练数据的方法。

Method: LiDAR-SAM2采用两阶段方案：(1) 数据侧，通过多视角投影和时空聚合将SAM2的视频掩码自动转换为时间上一致的LiDAR级标签；(2) 建模侧，设计定制化的模态接口和两阶段学习目标，使SAM2的视频分割核适配稀疏的时空LiDAR结构，通过单次点击即可获得跨序列一致的掩码轨迹。

Result: 在无任何人工LiDAR标注的情况下，LiDAR-SAM2在SemanticKITTI上生成的语义和全景标签仅需少量点即可接近全人工标注的质量，基于这些标签训练的模型性能接近使用完整真实标注训练的结果。

Conclusion: LiDAR-SAM2被定位为一种可扩展的标注工具，能大幅减少3D和4D场景理解中的人工标注负担，证明了从2D视频基础模型到4D LiDAR领域知识迁移的可行性。

Abstract: Progress in 4D LiDAR segmentation is bottlenecked by data. Assigning temporally consistent labels across sparse point cloud sequences is costly and hard to scale, and every new task or domain tends to demand fresh dense annotation. This motivates a simple question of whether high-quality LiDAR training data can be produced automatically, without any human labeling. To this end, we introduce LiDAR-SAM2, a framework that turns a 2D video foundation model, SAM2, into a scalable source of supervision for the 4D LiDAR domain. On the data side, it automatically generates temporally coherent LiDAR-level labels from SAM2 video masks through multi-view projection and spatio-temporal aggregation. On the modeling side, a tailored modality interface and a two-stage learning objective adapt SAM2's video segmentation kernel to spatio-temporal LiDAR structure, so that a single click per object yields a consistent mask track across the sequence. Trained with no human LiDAR annotation, LiDAR-SAM2 produces semantic and panoptic labels on SemanticKITTI that approach the quality of full human annotation from only a few points, and models trained on these labels approach the performance of full ground-truth supervision. This positions LiDAR-SAM2 as a scalable labeling tool that substantially reduces the annotation burden for 3D and 4D scene understanding.

</details>


### [29] [Saliency-Depth Conditioning for Zero-Shot Segmentation of Communication-Tower Components in Cluttered UAV Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25435)
*Ali Lesani,Chul Min Yeum,Su-Min Kang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模型无关的显著性-深度前景条件化策略，结合外观显著性与单目相对深度构建粗塔先验，抑制杂乱背景干扰，提升UAV通信塔图像中零样本分割模型的组件实例分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信塔组件细粒度分割对自动化巡检至关重要，但任务特定模型因实例级标注有限而难以训练。零样本分割模型在杂乱场景中易受视觉相似的背景结构干扰，导致漏检和误检。

Method: 提出模型无关的显著性-深度前景条件化策略，融合外观显著性与单目相对深度构建粗塔先验，抑制无关内容。将该模块与Grounded-SAM和SAM 3集成，得到SD-Grounded-SAM和SD-SAM 3。SD-Grounded-SAM在掩码生成前进一步应用几何与深度感知的框细化。

Result: 在TOW-300数据集（340张通信塔UAV图像）上，所提策略优于两个基线：SD-SAM 3在实例分割性能上表现最强，而SD-Grounded-SAM产生更少的误检。消融实验证实显著性、深度与框细化的互补增益，提升了杂乱场景的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 显著性-深度前景条件化策略能有效改善零样本分割模型在杂乱通信塔场景中的组件分割表现，为UAV自动化巡检提供了一种无需额外标注的通用增强方案。

Abstract: Fine-grained segmentation of communication-tower components in UAV imagery is essential for automated inspection, yet task-specific models are hard to develop due to limited instance-level annotations. Zero-shot segmentation models offer a promising alternative, but in cluttered scenes, visually similar background structures interfere with component localization, causing missed instances and false positives. We propose a model-agnostic saliency-depth foreground-conditioning strategy combining appearance-based saliency with monocular relative depth to construct a coarse tower prior and suppress irrelevant content. We integrate this module with Grounded-SAM and SAM 3, yielding SD-Grounded-SAM and SD-SAM 3. SD-Grounded-SAM further applies geometric and depth-aware box refinement before mask generation, while SD-SAM 3 relies on SAM 3's internal setup. On TOW-300, a dataset of 340 communication-tower UAV images, our strategy improves both baselines: SD-SAM 3 achieves the strongest instance-segmentation performance, while SD-Grounded-SAM produces fewer false positives. Ablations confirm complementary gains from saliency, depth, and box refinement, improving robustness in cluttered scenes.

</details>
