We propose CaSKG, a counterfactual-causal skill graph framework that calibrates procedural relations before retrieval. CaSKG first builds a high-recall directed candidate graph from semantic, lexical, input/output, and structural evidence, with repair evidence and an optional LLM judge further refining candidate scores. It then applies direction-conditioned textual counterfactual probes that remove, substitute, and reorder skill pairs, aggregates the evidence with Bayesian smoothing, and publishes a state-filtered weighted graph for task-conditioned expansion. The graph is constructed offline and used without changing the downstream agent policy or task interface. Across six LLM backbones on ALFWorld ID-140 and ScienceWorld U211, CaSKG achieves the highest task score in all twelve combinations of model and benchmark. Relative to Graph-of-Skills (GoS), it improves the six-model macro-average ScienceWorld score from 72.62 to 80.50 and ALFWorld success from 80.01\% to 86.79\%, while reducing mean environment steps on both benchmarks. Qualitative and ablation analyses further show that calibrated edges help retrieval preserve prerequisites, state-changing actions, verification routines, and final completion steps. These results position edge-confidence calibration as an effective route to compact and executable skill retrieval at scale\footnote{Code is available at: https://github.com/ZhiyuanLi218/Caskg }.

</details>


### [231] [Agentic Game Development as a Verifiable Trajectory Data Engine for Scaling World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25518)
*Pengfei Zhou,Hexin Wang,Zhengfeiyang Zhang,Yixing Ma,Zhenglin Wan,Kaipeng Zhang,Wangbo Zhao,Yang You*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出RLHEV（强化学习与人类-引擎验证）范式，利用游戏引擎作为空间世界模型的"可执行环境"，为RL后训练提供密实的奖励信号和真实的长时程轨迹数据，弥补当前基于CLIP等模糊代理指标的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界模型扩展主要依赖爬取更多视频和更多算力，这种方式效率低下。与代码智能体可以利用编译器/运行时提供高质量奖励不同，空间生成仍依赖CLIP分数等模糊且有偏的代理信号，难以支撑强化学习后训练。

Method: 提出'递归数据引擎'的概念，利用游戏引擎作为空间世界模型的奖励环境。场景被编码为可执行的游戏引擎世界规格，引擎可提供碰撞、物理、导航性和可玩性等密集检查信号，同时开发者通过判断场景是否被接受提供全局验证信号。最终构建RLHEV后训练范式，结合密集引擎信号与开发过程中隐含的人类接受反馈。

Result: 论文为概念性提议，提出RLHEV框架，论证了游戏开发作为空间世界模型奖励环境的可行性，认为其能提供比CLIP等模糊代理更可靠的质量信号，并包含真实世界长时程轨迹数据。

Conclusion: 游戏开发为空间世界模型提供了一种缺失的奖励环境，RLHEV范式有望使空间生成模型获得类似代码智能体在RL后训练上的成功，从而更高效地扩展世界模型。

Abstract: A common strategy for scaling world models is to train on more crawled video with more compute. We argue that this strategy is inefficient: scaling world models also requires a recursive data engine that offers grounded reward signals. The success of code agents illustrates why this matters. As code is executable, compilers and runtimes can provide high-quality rewards for Reinforcement Learning (RL) post-training of LLMs. By contrast, spatial generation still relies largely on fuzzy proxies such as CLIP scores. These signals are fuzzy and biased, making them hard to support RL post-training. Compared with these, game development provides a missing reward environment for spatial world models. A scene encoded by a game engine is an executable world specification: the engine can efficiently check collision, physics, navigability and bounded playability, while the developer provides the global verification signal by judging whether the scene should be accepted. Game development also provides real-world long-horizon trajectory data for RL post-training. We therefore propose Reinforcement Learning with Human-Engine Verification (RLHEV), a post-training paradigm that combines dense engine signals with implicit human acceptance feedback from the development process.

</details>


### [232] [Using profiles of cognitive capability to assess AI suitability for workplace tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25623)
*Jonathan Prunty,Marko Tešić,Patrick Quinn,José Hernández-Orallo,Lucy Cheke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于核心认知能力的AI任务分配框架，通过认知能力画像（从基准测试推断AI系统能力）和任务需求加权（从领域专家获取任务对认知能力的需求权重）两个独立模块，将AI系统与工作任务映射到共同认知维度空间，从而实现AI适应性的比较评估和人机任务分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在部署AI时面临任务分配难题（自动化、保留人工、人机协作），而现有聚合基准分数无法提供实际场景中的成功/失败洞察，人工判断模型能力又会迅速过时，亟需一种可更新、可比较的评估框架。

Method: 提出两条流水线：(1) 认知能力画像——从标注了认知需求的基准测试集中推断AI代理的能力；(2) 任务需求加权——从领域专家处获取相同认知能力对其工作的重要性权重。两者共享认知维度，可独立更新并组合以估算AI在不同粒度（领域、组织、角色、职责）的适应性。

Result: 在合成代理上验证了能力恢复的可靠性；对六个AI系统进行画像，发现AI系统在认知维度上的差异大于跨模型家族的差异；从六个职业领域的410名员工处获取了任务需求，发现工作活动收敛于共同的认知核心。最终分数可用于识别适合试点的AI候选及不适用领域。

Conclusion: 该框架为AI适宜性提供了比较性范围界定工具，并有望扩展至对人类员工的能力画像，从AI适宜性评估迈向人机任务分配。

Abstract: Organisations deploying AI face a scoping problem: which tasks can be automated, which should remain with humans, and which are best shared between the two. Aggregate benchmark scores provide little insight into where systems will succeed or fail in practice, while human judgements of model capabilities quickly become outdated. We introduce a pipeline that profiles agents and tasks using a shared set of core cognitive capabilities. Cognitive capability profiling infers an agent's capabilities from performance on a benchmark battery annotated for the cognitive demands of each item. Task requirements weighting elicits from domain experts the relative importance of these same capabilities for their work. As both use a common set of cognitive dimensions, they can be updated independently as models and roles change, and combined to estimate AI suitability at the level of a domain, organisation, role, or individual duty. We validate capability recovery on synthetic agents, profile six AI systems, and elicit task requirements from 410 employees across six occupational domains. AI systems differed more across cognitive dimensions than across model families, while workplace activities converged on a shared cognitive core. The resulting scores provide a comparative scoping tool for identifying promising candidates for piloting and areas where current systems are unlikely to be well suited. We discuss extending the framework to profile human workers alongside AI systems, moving from AI suitability towards human-machine task allocation.

</details>


### [233] [Narcissus: Program Synthesis Using Context-Aware LLM Approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25657)
*Tilman Hinnerichs,Sebastijan Dumancic,Neil Yorke-Smith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Narcissus是一个基于LLM引导的程序合成器，将LLM的提案保留为语法树而非规则频率，在候选程序的上下文中对每个展开进行评分，并通过正则化项保证每条语法规则可达，从而在固定目标语言的编程任务中优于静态引导和重新提示LLM的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM擅长编程，但当目标语言固定且语法在训练数据中罕见时，其生成的程序往往违反语法或无法满足规格。现有的枚举式合成器用LLM引导搜索时，将提案近似为规则频率会丢失结构上下文，并在提案错误时剪掉所有提案未涉及的规则，导致正确程序被隐藏。

Method: Narcissus将LLM提案保留为语法树形式，在候选程序展开时结合上下文进行评分：考察具有相同周围结构的提案是否以相同方式延续，以及展开是否重建了提案重复出现的片段。同时加入正则化项确保每条语法规则保持可达，使错误的提案只会延缓而非阻止正确解的发现。

Result: 在五个领域和两种搜索后端上，Narcissus在每个预算下都优于静态引导方法，并持续超越重新提示LLM修正自身提案的方法。它比原始提案快一个数量级到达类似提案的程序，在ARC任务上解决了40%的任务，而原始提案仅解决13%，且搜索过程中无需任何LLM调用。

Conclusion: 将LLM提案保留为语法树并在上下文感知的方式下引导枚举搜索，同时保证语法规则的完全可达性，是处理LLM不熟悉的罕见目标语言时高效且有效的程序合成策略。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel at programming, but not when the task fixes the target language: prompted with a grammar rare in their training data, their programs usually break the grammar or fail the given specification. Enumerative synthesizers search the space of syntactically correct programs systematically guided by LLMs; the state of the art guides them by approximating LLM proposals into rule frequencies, which loses where each construct belongs and prunes every rule the proposals miss, exactly when the proposals are wrong. We present Narcissus, a synthesizer that keeps the proposals as syntax trees and scores each expansion of a candidate program in its context: does a proposal with the same surrounding structure continue the same way, and does the expansion rebuild a fragment the proposals repeat? A regularization term keeps every rule reachable, so wrong proposals delay the solution but cannot hide it. Across five domains and two search backends, Narcissus beats static guidance at every budget and consistently outperforms re-prompting the LLM to fix its own proposals; it reaches proposal-like programs an order of magnitude sooner and solves $40\%$ of ARC tasks where the raw proposals solve $13\%$, all without a single LLM call during search.

</details>


### [234] [ToST: A Tree-of-Thought Socratic Teaching Framework for Multi-Path Guidance and Parallel Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25775)
*Feng Ling,Heng Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ToST框架，一种基于思维树（Tree-of-Thought）的苏格拉底式教学框架，支持"一题多解"的多路径引导教学范式，并构建了包含31K多路径教学对话的MPSG-Bench基准，以系统评估非线性教学能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型驱动的苏格拉底式教学通常采用"一题一解"范式，将教学引导限制在单一线性推理路径上，导致教学灵活性不足、错误恢复能力弱，且无法支持学生进行并行思维探索多种有效解决方案。

Method: 提出ToST（Tree-of-Thought Socratic Teaching）框架，包含两个核心机制：1）Parallel Sowing——面向并行思维的提问策略，鼓励学生从多角度探索问题；2）Multi-Path Adaptive Guidance——跨多条备选解答轨迹提供更稳健、非线性的教学指导。同时构建MPSG-Bench基准，包含31K多路径教学对话数据集和基于SOLO理论的五维评估框架。

Result: 在自动评估和人工评估指标下，ToST显著提升了教学引导成功率，并更有效地赋能学生探索和导航多条解题路径。

Conclusion: ToST通过引入思维树机制将苏格拉底式教学从单路径推向多路径范式，填补了多路径教学引导系统性评估的空白，为基于LLM的智能教学系统提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) exhibit strong problem-solving abilities, positioning them as promising agents for Socratic teaching to guide students through step-by-step heuristic questioning. However, existing approaches typically adopt a one-problem-one-solution paradigm, restricting the teaching guidance to a single linear reasoning path. This design limits instructional flexibility, weakens error recovery, and restricts students' ability to engage in parallel thinking to explore multiple valid solutions. To overcome these, we propose ToST, a Tree-of-Thought Socratic Teaching framework that explicitly supports multi-path guidance under a one-problem-multiple-solutions paradigm. ToST employs Parallel Sowing, a parallel-thinking-oriented questioning strategy to encourage students to approach problems from diverse perspectives, and a Multi-Path Adaptive Guidance mechanism to provide more robust and non-linear instructions across alternative solution trajectories. Concurrently, to fill the void in systematically evaluating such non-linear instructional capabilities, we advance the task of multi-path Socratic guidance by establishing MPSG-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark that includes a dataset of 31K multi-path teaching dialogues and a five-dimensional evaluation framework grounded in the SOLO (Structure of Observed Learning Outcomes) theory to assess parallel-thinking guidance. Experimental results demonstrate that ToST significantly enhances guidance success rates while empowering students to navigate and explore multiple solution paths more effectively under both automatic and human metrics.

</details>


### [235] [LocalLSTC: A Long Short-Term Control Architecture for Locally Deployed GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25777)
*Weiming Li,Helen Paik,Yulei Sui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LocalLSTC，一种无需训练的GUI-agent架构，通过按时间范围组织控制信息——长期控制维护跨步骤的持久状态，短期执行实现当前步骤的有界操作——来缓解弱推理模型下控制信息隐式导致的任务失败。在OSWorld和WindowsAgentArena上超越先前最强本地模型结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代GUI-agent框架依赖前沿API模型获得强性能，但在弱本地推理模型下性能大幅下降（GPT-5换为Qwen3.5-9B后OSWorld SR-100从60.9%降至37.7%）。原因在于控制信息在漫长的交互轨迹中往往隐式存在，规划器每步需重建任务阶段、累积证据和运行时反馈，这种依赖在弱推理模型下更加严重。轨迹标注显示91.6%的失败轨迹存在至少一个控制失败。

Method: LocalLSTC是一种免训练的架构，通过时间范围组织控制信息。Long-Term Control维护活跃子目标、子目标对齐证据和跨交互的运行时反馈；Short-Term Execution在当前步骤实现有界操作承诺；Long-to-Short Planning从持久状态形成每个操作承诺；Short-to-Long Control将执行结果整合回持久状态，用于进度评估、恢复和终止。

Result: 使用Qwen3.6-27B，LocalLSTC在OSWorld上达到64.7% SR-100，在WindowsAgentArena上达到65.3%，均优于先前最强的本地模型结果。消融实验进一步支持执行两侧机制的贡献。

Conclusion: 控制信息的时间组织是本地部署GUI-agent的一个独立架构维度，LocalLSTC通过区分长期控制与短期执行改进了弱推理模型下的任务完成性能。

Abstract: Modern GUI-agent frameworks achieve strong desktop task performance with frontier API models, yet persistent control information often remains implicit in growing interaction trajectories. At each step, the planner reconstructs the active task stage, accumulated evidence, and runtime feedback before deciding the next action. This dependence becomes more pronounced under weaker local reasoning backbones. Across four representative state-of-the-art frameworks, replacing GPT-5 with Qwen3.5-9B reduces average OSWorld SR-100 from 60.9\% to 37.7\%. Trajectory annotation further identifies at least one control failure in 91.6\% of failed trajectories. To address this problem, we introduce LocalLSTC, a training-free architecture that organizes control by temporal scope, maintaining persistent cross-step state to guide short-term execution commitments. Long-Term Control maintains the active subgoal, subgoal-aligned evidence, and runtime feedback across interactions, while Short-Term Execution realizes bounded commitments for the current step. Long-to-Short Planning forms each commitment from persistent state, and Short-to-Long Control integrates execution outcomes back into that state for progress assessment, recovery, and termination. With Qwen3.6-27B, LocalLSTC reaches 64.7\% SR-100 on OSWorld and 65.3\% on WindowsAgentArena, outperforming the strongest prior local results on both benchmarks. Ablations further support contributions from mechanisms on both sides of execution. These findings identify temporal organization of control information as a distinct architectural dimension for locally deployed GUI agents.

</details>


### [236] [Choose Your Game Wisely: Measuring Game-Theoretic Structures in Real-World Vehicle Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25917)
*Yueyuan Li,Rongcheng Nie,Weijie Xi,Mingyang Jiang,Songan Zhang,Hanyang Zhuang,Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于轨迹的交互测量框架，用于从真实驾驶数据中识别交互事件并量化行为变化起始、时间组织、响应动态和排序稳定性。在六个真实轨迹数据集上的评估表明，并发和序贯行为变化均占显著比例，稳定排序比交替排序更普遍，且时间顺序不一定伴随着可测量的行为响应。因此，不同的博弈论建模公式应被视为互补的抽象工具，而非适用于所有车辆交互的通用结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 博弈论模型为车辆交互提供了原则性的建模框架，但其潜在的时间假设（同时、顺序和不对称交互结构）尚未在实际驾驶行为中得到系统性检验，即如何从车辆轨迹中测量这些交互结构仍然不清楚。

Method: 开发了一个基于轨迹的交互测量框架：首先识别交互事件，然后量化行为变化起始时间、时间组织方式、响应动态以及排序稳定性。该框架利用行为偏差来验证候选交互，并在六个真实轨迹数据集（INTERACTION、highD、inD、rounD、Waymo Open Motion和nuPlan）上进行了评估，覆盖多种道路几何、交通环境和交互类型。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 并发和序贯行为变化在跟驰、并线和冲突交互中均占显著比例；(2) 在序贯交互中，稳定排序比交替排序更普遍，表明持久的非对称角色是常见交互结构；(3) 时间上先发生的响应并不一定伴随可测量的行为反应，说明时间排序本身不足以表征行为依赖关系。

Conclusion: 真实世界中的车辆交互表现出并发、序贯和持续有序等多种时间结构。因此，不同的博弈论公式不应被视为普适的统一定义，而应视为针对不同交互机制的互补建模抽象。

Abstract: Game-theoretic models provide principled frameworks for modeling vehicle interactions, but their underlying temporal assumptions have not been systematically examined against real-world driving behavior. In particular, it remains unclear how simultaneous, sequential, and asymmetric interaction structures can be measured from vehicle trajectories. This paper develops a trajectory-based interaction measurement framework to identify interaction events and quantify behavioral change onset, temporal organization, post-onset response dynamics, and ordering stability. The framework uses behavioral deviations to verify candidate interactions. We evaluate the framework on six real-world trajectory datasets, including INTERACTION, highD, inD, rounD, Waymo Open Motion, and nuPlan, covering diverse road geometries, traffic environments, and interaction types. The results show that concurrent and sequential behavioral changes both constitute substantial proportions of observed following, merging, and conflicting interactions. Among sequential interactions, stable ordering is more prevalent than alternating ordering, indicating that persistent asymmetric roles are a common interaction structure. Importantly, temporal precedence does not necessarily coincide with a measurable behavioral response, indicating that temporal ordering alone may not be sufficient to characterize behavioral dependence. These findings show that real-world interactions exhibit concurrent, sequential, and persistently ordered temporal structures. Different game-theoretic formulations are therefore better regarded as complementary modeling abstractions for different interaction regimes rather than as a universal structure governing all vehicle interactions.

</details>


### [237] [Repair or Resample? Rethinking Failure Debugging in LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25920)
*Zhongwen Luan,Xiaoyu Zhang,Ming Hu,Yue Yang,Jiongchi Yu,Xiaohong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SymTrace，一个受控评估框架，用于检验基于LLM的多智能体系统（MAS）修复方法是否真正因果性地修复故障，而非仅依赖LLM采样的随机性。作者构建了包含536条人工标注故障轨迹的数据集SymFail，并在三个主流MAS框架上进行大规模实证研究，发现现有无引导重跑方法不可靠（故障复现率仅67.97%，修复率仅6.90%），而基于症状驱动的干预方法可将修复率提升至20.15%，较SOTA方法提升191.89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM多智能体系统在长时程复杂任务中的广泛应用，其可靠性成为实际部署的核心瓶颈。现有MAS调试和修复方法通常依赖对整个执行轨迹的重跑和重采样，但一个根本问题悬而未决：这些方法到底是因果性地修复了MAS故障，还是仅仅通过利用LLM采样的随机性实现了随机性修复？这个问题直接关系到修复方法的真实有效性，因此需要一个受控的评估框架来回答。

Method: 本文提出SymTrace框架，记录MAS执行轨迹并建立干预锚点，在重放时利用记录日志重建锚点之前的执行，仅重新生成锚点之后的下游轨迹，从而可靠复现MAS故障。同时构建了SymFail数据集，包含536条人工标注的故障轨迹，配有图链接位置、故障类别和追踪证据。基于这些基础设施，在三个主流MAS框架上开展大规模实证研究，对比现有无引导重跑方法与症状驱动的干预修复方法。

Result: 实证研究发现：现有无引导重跑方法高度不可靠，故障复现率仅为67.97%，修复率仅为6.90%；而基于症状驱动的干预修复方法成功修复了20.15%的失败案例，相比现有最先进的修复方法提升了191.89%。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了现有MAS修复方法的根本缺陷——它们可能只是利用LLM采样随机性的随机性修复而非真正的因果修复，并提供了一套受控评估框架（SymTrace）和数据集（SymFail）以及更有效的症状驱动干预方法，为MAS调试与修复研究提供了可操作的洞见，推动多智能体系统的稳健部署。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MASs) are increasingly applied to long-horizon complex tasks, their reliability has emerged as the core bottleneck hindering their real-world deployment. Existing MAS debugging and repair methods typically rely on rerunning and resampling the entire execution trajectory. However, a fundamental question remains to be answered: do these methods causally repair MAS failures or merely stochastically repair by leveraging the randomness of LLM sampling? To evaluate the effectiveness of MAS repair methods, we introduce SymTrace, a controlled evaluation framework that records the MAS execution trajectory and establishes intervention anchors. During replay, it effectively reconstructs the execution before the anchor using recorded logs and only regenerates the downstream trajectory, thereby enabling the reliable reproduction of MAS failures. We further construct the dataset SymFail, comprising 536 human-annotated failure trajectories with graph-linked locations, categories, and trace evidence. Based on these foundations, we conduct a large-scale empirical study across three mainstream MAS frameworks. Our findings reveal that existing unguided rerun methods are highly unreliable, exhibiting low failure reproduction and repair rates (only 67.97% and 6.90%, respectively). Building upon these findings, we further explore the effectiveness of a symptom-driven intervention method, which successfully repairs 20.15% of the failed cases (a 191.89% improvement to state-of-the-art repair methods). This study aims to provide actionable insights for MAS debugging and repair research, paving the way for the robust deployment of multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [238] [Formal, Executable and Explainable Runtime Monitoring of Spoken Air Traffic Control Operational Procedures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25926)
*Roberto Luvini,Giacomo Longo,Alessandro Armando,Enrico Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个运行时验证框架，通过解析管制员与飞行员之间的无线电通话、监视数据和机载观测数据，自动监控空中交通管制程序的执行情况，检测指令是否被正确遵循。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中交通管制程序通过管制员与飞行员的口头交流执行，这些交互对航空安全至关重要，执行失败可能导致严重事故。需要自动化评估指令是否被遵循，但需要关联通话内容、航空器状态及飞行员义务。

Method: 框架将无线电通信解析为关联到相关实体的事件，并与监视数据和机载观测合并为带时间戳的轨迹。将ICAO衍生的义务形式化为具有显式时间边界的时序公式，在执行轨迹上进行评估，报告每条违规及支持其裁决的观测。

Result: 在真实交通数据上，完整流程对人工标注的违规检测F1值达到0.85；在来自两个公共语料库的1,495个合成场景中，监控逻辑在所有情况下都返回预期裁决；在根据官方调查报告重建的两个历史事故中，监控器识别出了与调查人员记录的相同的程序偏离。

Conclusion: 该运行时验证框架能够有效监控空中交通管制程序执行，在真实数据、合成场景和历史事故重建中均展现出良好的检测性能与可靠性。

Abstract: Air traffic control procedures are executed through spoken exchanges between controllers and pilots. These interactions are essential to the safety of air transportation: failures in their execution can create severe operational hazards, as evidenced by past fatal accidents. Assessing whether an instruction has been followed requires relating what was said to the aircraft concerned, its state, and the obligations that pilots must meet. We present a runtime verification framework that monitors such procedures by checking controller-pilot exchanges, surveillance data, and onboard observations. The framework parses radio communications into events linked to the entities they concern and merges them with surveillance and onboard observations into a time-stamped trace. The ICAO-derived obligations as formalized as temporal formulas with explicit time bounds and evaluated over execution traces. Every violation is reported along with the breached obligations and the observations that support the verdict. With real traffic, the complete pipeline reaches an F1 of 0.85 against blind human-annotated violations; in 1,495 synthetic situations derived from two public corpora, the monitor logic returns the expected verdict in every case. In two historical accidents reconstructed from official investigation reports, the monitor identifies the same procedural deviations documented by the investigators.

</details>


### [239] [How Robust Are Automated Fact-Checking Systems? A Cross-Benchmark Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25934)
*Aida Usmanova,Zangir Iklassov,Markus Leippold,Ricardo Usbeck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次对自动化事实核查（AFC）的完整"检索-验证"两阶段流程进行跨数据集基准测试，评估了9个模型在科学、开放网络和气候三个领域4个数据集上的表现，发现模型排名高度依赖领域和评估指标，且证据检索质量是当前AFC系统的首要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AFC评估存在两大缺陷：一是忽略了简单基线模型；二是系统往往只在单一基准上开发，跨领域泛化能力未被检验。此前的研究只分别评估了检索阶段或单阶段验证，缺乏对完整两阶段流水线的跨数据集交叉评估。

Method: 构建了一个系统化的跨数据集基准框架，涵盖9个模型（从随机和稀疏基线、微调Transformer、零样本LLM，到AVeriTeC 2025共享任务中排名最高的两个系统），在4个涉及科学、开放网络和气候领域的数据集上评估完整的检索-验证流水线。

Result: 三个关键发现：(1) 在ClimateCheck上，仅使用声明和微调模型优于零样本LLM和AVeriTeC 2025顶级系统，说明噪声证据会损害真实性预测；(2) 模型排名强烈依赖领域和评估指标，最佳模型在SciFact上macro-F1为0.70，但在ClimateCheck上骤降至0.31；(3) 用人工标注的金标准证据替换检索证据可将准确率提升14-22个百分点，证实检索是主要瓶颈。

Conclusion: 现有AFC系统跨领域泛化能力不足，模型评估必须考虑领域和指标的多样性；证据检索质量是制约整体性能的最关键环节，未来研究应优先改进检索模块。同时作者开源了代码和数据集以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Automated fact-checking (AFC) systems retrieve evidence and predict claim veracity, yet evaluations omit simple baselines, systems are developed for a single benchmark and cannot be trusted to generalise across domains. No prior work cross-evaluates the full two-stage retrieve-then-verify pipeline across diverse datasets, complementing retrieval-only studies (Thakur et al., 2021) and single-stage benchmarking studies (Calamai et al., 2025). We benchmark nine models, ranging from random and sparse baselines to fine-tuned transformers, zero-shot LLMs, and the two highest-ranked systems from the AVeriTeC 2025 shared task, across four datasets spanning scientific, open-web, and climate domains. Three findings stand out: (1) on ClimateCheck claim-only and fine-tuned models outperform zero-shot LLM and top-performing AVeriTeC 2025 systems, highlighting that noisy evidence can degrade veracity prediction; (2) system rankings are strongly domain- and metric-dependent: the best model on SciFact (macro-F1 0.70) drops to 0.31 on ClimateCheck, while the AVeriTeC 2025 winner and runner-up swap rankings based on evaluation metrics and datasets; (3) replacing retrieved evidence with gold annotations improves veracity accuracy by 14-22 points across models, confirming retrieval remains primary bottleneck. We release code, pre-processed datasets, and all results to support reproducible AFC research.

</details>


### [240] [Candidate supply and answer selection shape the value of LLM judging in multi-agent systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25937)
*Jia-Hao Ji,Sijie Li,Jiabei Cheng,Zixi She,Jin-Tai Yu,Zhiyuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了多智能体系统（MAS）中正确但未被报告的答案问题，将其概念化为"候选生成-同伴通信-终端选择"的进化管线，类比"迷因漂移"现象。通过分析15,336个问题和重放81,390个固定候选池，发现正确答案常已存在于候选中但系统仍会收敛到错误答案；法官（LLM judge）可靠性随任务、生成器和答案稀有度变化；结合答案频率与法官评估可将准确率从63.82%提升至70.82-70.95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统有时已具备正确回答的潜力，但最终仍报告错误答案。由于生成、通信和最终答案选择规则通常同时变化，解释这一结果非常困难。作者希望隔离这些环节，建立诊断性基础以指导多智能体架构设计。

Method: 作者将多智能体推理建模为候选生成、同伴通信和终端选择的三阶段进化管线，分析LLM法官提供答案正确性信号时的选择压力。使用了15,336个问题（来自MMLU-Pro、GPQA、MedXpertQA和MuSR，HLE单独分析），并通过重放16,278个问题中的81,390个固定候选池来测试规则。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）正确答案常已存在于生成候选中，但系统仍可能收敛到并报告错误答案；（2）法官可靠性并非模型的固定特质，而是随任务、生成器和正确答案稀有度变化；（3）将答案频率与法官评估结合仅改变最终答案选择规则，将准确率从63.82%提升至70.82-70.95%，主要通过挽救被流行错误数量压制但正确的答案。

Conclusion: 通过隔离生成、识别和选择三个环节，本文为设计能够保护生成正确答案不被丢失的多智能体架构提供了诊断性基础。生成更多候选的价值取决于这些额外样本是否使正确答案变得存在、频繁或可被识别。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) sometimes already have the potential to answer correctly, but still report a wrong answer. Explaining this outcome is difficult because generation, communication and final answer-selection rules usually change simultaneously. We conceptualize multi-agent reasoning as an evolutionary pipeline of candidate generation, peer communication and terminal selection, wherein consensus without quality control can exhibit patterns of memetic drift. We study two questions: (1) when an LLM judge provides effective selection pressure by supplying a signal of answer correctness for candidates generated in a multi-agent system, and (2) when using that signal improves the reported answer. To map judge reliability, we analysed 15,336 questions from MMLU-Pro, GPQA, MedXpertQA and MuSR, with Humanity's Last Exam analysed separately. To test these rules, we replayed 81,390 fixed candidate pools drawn from 16,278 questions across five benchmarks. We report three findings. (1) A correct answer is often already present among the generated candidates, but the system can still converge on and report a wrong answer. (2) Judge reliability is not a fixed trait of the model, but varies with the task, the generator and how rare the correct answer is. (3) Combining answer frequency with the judge's evaluation changed only the final answer-selection rule and raised accuracy from 63.82% to 70.82-70.95%, primarily by rescuing correct answers that were outnumbered by popular errors. In the systems studied here, the value of generating more candidates depends on whether those extra samples make correct answers present, frequent or recognisable. By isolating generation, recognition and selection, these findings establish a diagnostic basis for designing multi-agent architectures that protect generated correct answers from being lost.

</details>


### [241] [LivingRAG: Augmenting Graph RAG with Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25960)
*Yuzhuo Cui,Zongye Zhang,Qingjie Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LivingRAG是一个图RAG框架，通过可写可复用的推理经验存储来提升多跳问答性能。它在图检索骨干上增加经验存储，将已验证的推理经验（图信号和摘要）复用于后续相关查询，从而避免从零开始检索和推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统每次查询孤立处理，丢弃了LLM推理后产生的有用推理经验，导致后续相关查询需要重新检索证据并从零推理，效率低下且浪费计算资源。

Method: LivingRAG在基于图的检索骨干上增加可写经验存储。推理经验通过两种方式复用：1) 存储的图信号帮助检索找到早期相关查询中有用的实体和段落；2) 存储的摘要为答案生成提供参考推理模式。该方法还分析了在线QA流中可复用的信号来源，包括共享实体、图邻域和问题模板。

Result: 在多跳QA基准上的实验表明，LivingRAG相比强RAG基线提升了准确率，并在复用相关先前经验时减少了完成token的使用量。

Conclusion: 通过对推理经验的可写和可复用设计，LivingRAG能够有效利用LLM推理过程中产生的有价值信息，在提高问答准确性的同时降低计算开销，证明了跨查询推理经验复用对RAG系统的重要性。

Abstract: Graph-based RAG improves multi-hop question answering by organizing evidence as a knowledge graph. However, most existing RAG systems process each query in isolation and discard useful reasoning from the LLM's response after inference. As a result, later related queries need to retrieve evidence and reason from scratch. We propose LivingRAG, a Graph RAG framework with writable and reusable reasoning experience. LivingRAG adds a writable experience store to a graph-based retrieval backbone, enabling verified experiences to be reused during inference in two ways. Stored graph signals help retrieval find entities and passages that were useful in earlier related queries. Stored summaries provide a reference reasoning pattern for answer generation. We analyze online QA streams and find reusable signals from shared entities, graph neighborhoods, and question templates. Experiments on multi-hop QA benchmarks show that LivingRAG improves accuracy over strong RAG baselines and reduces completion-token use when relevant prior experience is reused.

</details>


### [242] [Quantitative Analysis of $ω$-Regular Robust MDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25968)
*Ali Asadi,Krishnendu Chatterjee,Ehsan Kafshdar Goharshady,Mehrdad Karrabi,Alipasha Montaseri,Ali Shafiee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有线性定义不确定性集的(s,a)-矩形鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程（RMDPs）中奇偶性目标的定量问题，提出了纯记忆无记忆最优策略的证明、多项式时间算法及基于策略迭代的求解方法，并通过实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往工作仅解决了RMDPs中奇偶性目标的定性分析（几乎必然满足或正概率满足），而定量精确值问题尚未解决，这是该领域的关键空白。

Method: 首先证明智能体和环境均存在纯记忆无记忆最优策略；其次为线性定义的鲁棒马尔可夫链设计多项式时间定量奇偶性算法，并将其用作策略迭代算法的子程序；所提算法结合了定量单步改进与定性几乎必然改进。

Result: 提出了多项式时间算法用于线性定义鲁棒马尔可夫链的定量奇偶性分析，并给出了RMDPs上的策略迭代算法；实验对比了与显式归约到随机博弈的方法。

Conclusion: 该工作首次解决了线性定义不确定性集下RMDPs奇偶性目标的精确定量问题，理论结果与实验均验证了所提方法的有效性。

Abstract: Robust Markov Decision Processes (RMDPs) generalize classical MDPs by allowing uncertainty in transition probabilities and optimizing against their worst-case realization. We consider $(s,a)$-rectangular RMDPs with \emph{linearly defined} uncertainty sets and study parity objectives, which are a canonical representation of $ω$-regular objectives. An uncertainty set is linearly defined if it is described by linear inequalities over the transition distribution together with auxiliary variables, which capture the standard $L_1$ and $L_\infty$ balls as well as general polytopic uncertainty sets.
  The quantitative value is the supremum, over all agent policies, of the satisfaction probability guaranteed against the adversarial environment. Previous work studied the qualitative analysis, namely the almost-sure (resp. positive) problem that asks whether a single agent policy guarantees satisfaction with probability one (resp. positive probability) against every environment policy. In this work, we solve the exact quantitative problem.
  Our contributions are threefold. First, we show that both the agent and the environment admit pure memoryless optimal policies. Second, we give a polynomial-time algorithm for quantitative parity on linearly defined robust Markov chains and use it as a subroutine in a policy-iteration algorithm for RMDPs. The algorithm combines quantitative one-step improvements with qualitative almost-sure improvements. Finally, we report experiments comparing our approach with the explicit reduction to stochastic games.

</details>


### [243] [SciMIF: Understanding Multimodal Instruction Following in Scientific Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25973)
*Ye Shen,Yuting Zheng,Dun Pei,Zijian Chen,Wenlong Zhang,Qi Jia,Guangtao Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SciMIF基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在科学领域中遵循复杂指令的能力，涵盖22个任务、5个科学学科和10个约束组，实验发现化学学科挑战最大、模型规模增大不带来指令遵循提升、细粒度约束仍是难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学领域中指令遵循能力对MLLMs在科学研究中的应用至关重要，但目前缺乏针对多模态指令遵循的系统性评估基准。

Method: 提出包含10个约束组的综合分类体系，覆盖通用功能需求和学科特定特征；开发高保真指令注入流水线，系统增强现有科学数据集；构建SciMIF基准。

Result: 实验显示各学科性能差异显著，化学最具挑战性；增大模型规模并未带来约束遵循的相应改进；当前模型在细粒度约束和需深度应用学科知识的指令上仍需突破。

Conclusion: SciMIF填补了科学领域多模态指令遵循评估的空白，为未来MLLMs在严谨科学应用中的改进奠定了基础。

Abstract: Understanding instruction-following capabilities in scientific domains is essential for effectively leveraging Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to advance the development of scientific fields. In this work, we introduce SciMIF, a novel benchmark designed to evaluate the capability of MLLMs in following complex scientific instructions. Specifically, based on an extensive analysis of 22 distinct tasks across 5 representative scientific disciplines, we propose a comprehensive taxonomy comprising 10 constraint groups that captures both general functional requirements and discipline-specific characteristics. Guided by this taxonomy, we develop a high-fidelity instruction injection pipeline to systematically augment existing scientific datasets. We conduct comprehensive experiments on multiple state-of-the-art closed-source and open-source MLLMs. Our findings reveal significant performance disparities across different scientific disciplines, with chemistry posing greater challenges for current MLLMs. Furthermore, we observe that increasing the model scale does not yield corresponding improvements in constraint adherence, and current models still struggle severely with fine-grained constraints and instructions requiring the deep application of disciplinary knowledge. SciMIF fills the current void in evaluating multimodal instruction adherence within scientific domains, laying a crucial foundation for future enhancements of MLLMs in rigorous scientific applications. Data and code will be released at https://github.com/shenye7436/SciMIF .

</details>


### [244] [Multi-Granularity Context-Enhanced RAG over Multimodal Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25986)
*Zongyu Wu,Yilong Wang,Xiaochen Wang,Minhua Lin,Zhichao Xu,Fenglong Ma,Xiang Zhang,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CEMMKG框架，通过为多模态知识图谱中的每张图片补充局部和全局范围的文本上下文信息（局部上下文包含语义相关的句子，全局上下文提供段落摘要），并引入多粒度设计，以缩小图文语义鸿沟，从而提升基于多模态知识图谱的GraphRAG性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多模态知识图谱（MMKG）的RAG方法通常在不同模态独立处理后进行融合，导致文本上下文在视觉信息提取和多模态知识融合阶段利用有限，造成图像与文本之间的语义鸿沟，限制了多模态GraphRAG的性能。

Method: 提出上下文增强的多模态知识图谱（CEMMKG）构建框架：为每张图片在局部和全局两个范围补充文本上下文。局部上下文包含与图像语义相关的句子（超越周边文本），并采用多粒度设计以在不同细节层面捕获语义相关信息；全局上下文提供整个段落的摘要。

Result: 在视觉中心的数据集上的大量实验验证了CEMMKG能有效利用上下文信息提升基于MMKG的RAG性能，且在不同MMKG-RAG方法上的有效性证明了其广泛的适用性。

Conclusion: CEMMKG通过构建上下文增强的多模态知识图谱，有效弥合了图像与文本之间的语义鸿沟，显著提升了多模态GraphRAG的性能，并具有良好的跨方法泛化能力。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is widely used to mitigate hallucination issues in large language models (LLMs) and multimodal large language models (MLLMs). In particular, knowledge graph (KG)-based RAG leverages structured knowledge to provide (M)LLMs with high-quality external information. Building on these works, recent studies have explored multimodal knowledge graphs (MMKGs) as knowledge bases for GraphRAG. This enables Graph RAG to integrate knowledge across multiple modalities, thereby further enhancing its performance. However, existing MMKG-based RAG methods generally follow a common pipeline in which different modalities are largely processed independently before being fusion. As a result, textual context is only used to a limited extent during visual information extraction and subsequent multimodal knowledge fusion. This brings a semantic gap between images and text which limits the multimodal GraphRAG performance. To address this issue, we propose a novel framework for constructing a Context-Enhanced MMKG (CEMMKG) to better support multimodal GraphRAG. The proposed CEMMKG enriches each image with complementary textual context at both local and global scopes. Local context goes beyond the surrounding text by incorporating sentences that are semantically related to the image, while global context provides a summary of the entire passage. We further introduce a multi-granularity design for the local context, allowing it to capture semantically relevant information at different levels of detail. Extensive experiments on the selected vision-centric dataset validate that CEMMKG is effective in leveraging contextual information to improve MMKG-based RAG performance. Moreover, its effectiveness across different MMKG-based RAG methods demonstrates its broad applicability.

</details>


### [245] [ProgRouter: Online Progress-Guided Orchestration for Multi-Agent LLM Workflows under Quality-Cost Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25992)
*Somgyuan Li,Ahmed M. Abdelmoniem,Shiqiang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProgRouter是一个在线进度引导路由框架，在多智能体LLM工作流中自适应选择最优LLM代理，在保持任务求解质量的同时降低运营成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体LLM工作流推理成本高昂，而现有的级联路由方法仅做一次性查询级决策，无法适应多步工作流的动态、状态依赖特性——每一步应选择哪个LLM取决于任务进展、剩余难度和成本效率要求。

Method: ProgRouter包含三个核心组件：(1)多视图任务进度评分器，结合粗粒度工作流结果区间与细粒度子任务完成度、进度趋势和工作流状态质量信号；(2)双路径任务进度预测器；(3)自适应元门控机制，估算每个候选LLM的进度增益。然后基于进度增益、时间预算和长期成本效率做出在线逐步路由决策。

Result: 在HumanEval Plus、MBPP、MATH-500和ASQA四个基准上（覆盖智能体代码生成、数学推理和检索增强长文问答），ProgRouter相比关键基线降低了运营成本，同时保持强大的任务求解性能。

Conclusion: ProgRouter验证了在线进度引导的路由方法能够有效平衡任务质量、时间预算与运营成本效率，优于传统的一次性级联路由方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) workflows have emerged as a powerful paradigm for solving complex, open-ended tasks through collaborative reasoning among specialized LLM agents, but they incur substantial operating costs due to repeated LLM invocations and long-horizon context accumulation. Existing cascade routing methods make one-shot, query-level decisions and cannot adapt to the dynamic, state-dependent nature of multi-step workflows, in which the right LLM at each step depends on evolving task progress, remaining task difficulty, and cost-efficiency requirements. We present ProgRouter, an online progress-guided routing framework that adaptively selects LLM agents across workflow steps to preserve task-solving quality while adhering to time and cost budgets. ProgRouter introduces a multi-view task progress scorer that combines coarse workflow outcome regimes with fine-grained signals on subtask completion, progress trends, and workflow state quality. Then, a dual-path task progress predictor and an adaptive meta-gating mechanism estimate the progress gain for each candidate routed LLM. ProgRouter makes online step-wise routing decisions that balance progress gain, task time budgets, and long-term operating cost efficiency. Experiments on HumanEval Plus, MBPP, MATH-500, and ASQA, spanning agentic code generation, mathematical reasoning, and retrieval-augmented long-form question answering, demonstrate that ProgRouter reduces the operating cost relative to key baselines while maintaining strong task-solving performance.

</details>


### [246] [AsymSpec: Context-Asymmetric Speculative Decoding for Agentic LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26004)
*Sheng Liang,Yongyue Zhang,Nathanael Brian,Hang Lv,Hao Wang,Chen Zhang,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AsymSpec提出一种非对称投机解码框架：轻量级drafter读取完整输入，大型验ber器操¬作压缩视图，通过对比δ-logit融合和发散感知接受门控，在保留约90%全上下文准确率的同时获得1.3-1.7倍吞吐提升，计算成本仅为原先的0.2-0.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic LLM流水线因上下文积累导致推理成本激增，现有压缩手段虽能控制延迟但损害任务准确率。投机解码（SD）虽能无损加速，但其前提假设drafter与verifier共享相同上下文，无法解决准确率与开销之间的权衡问题。

Method: 打破SD中drafter与verifier上下文对称的假设：轻量级drafter读取完整输入，大型verifier操作压缩视图。drafter通过对比δ融合（contrastive δ-fusion）的logits引导verifier，并由发散感知接受门控（divergence-aware acceptance gate）调制，以保持验证稳定性并实现高草稿接受率。

Result: 在四个agentic能力和两个端到端agent基准上评估：平均达到约90%的全上下文准确率，在隔离文本能力上实现1.3-1.7倍吞吐加速，计算成本仅为原始方案的0.2-0.3倍。

Conclusion: 非对称上下文访问策略在压缩丢弃关键推理信号时能带来显著收益，证明通过架构层面的对称性打破可以同时改善准确率与推理效率。

Abstract: Agentic LLM pipelines face escalating inference costs as context accumulates across retrieval, tool use, and multi-turn interactions. To control latency, deployments routinely compress inputs, but this degrades task accuracy. Speculative decoding (SD) accelerates generation losslessly, yet it assumes the drafter and verifier share an identical context, preventing SD from resolving the accuracy-overhead trade-off. We propose AsymSpec, an asymmetric speculative decoding framework that breaks this symmetry: a lightweight drafter reads the full input while the large verifier operates on the compressed view. The drafter steers the verifier via a contrastive $δ$-fusion of logits, modulated by a divergence-aware acceptance gate that preserves verification stability and high draft acceptance rates. Evaluated across four agentic capabilities and two end-to-end agent benchmarks, AsymSpec reaches $\approx 90\%$ of full-context accuracy on average, delivering $1.3$--$1.7\times$ throughput speedups at $0.2$--$0.3\times$ the compute cost on isolated text capabilities. These results show that asymmetric context access yields substantial gains precisely when compression discards critical reasoning signals.

</details>


### [247] [Imitation Learning for Connection-Tableau Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26009)
*Fredrik Rømming,Mantas Bakšys,Martin S. Fixman,Sean B. Holden*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将自动定理证明器构建证明的过程视为一个基于形式演算诱导出的转移系统中的策略（policy），该系统覆盖了leanCoP风格搜索和plCoP/rlCoP风格规划，并提供统一接口供策略学习方法直接应用。通过图神经网络对证明编辑打分，采用模仿学习训练，并系统性地移除搜索脚手架（从完全符号回溯到纯网络驱动策略）来衡量性能。在M2k、MPTP2078-bushy和TPTP v9.2.1基准上，学习策略相比leanCoP最多多解决46%的问题，且达到证明所需步数少一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动定理证明器在构建证明时逐步选择添加和删除的内容，但不同搜索架构（如leanCoP风格搜索与plCoP/rlCoP风格规划）之间缺乏统一的策略学习接口，限制了策略学习方法的应用与跨问题泛化。

Method: 定义了一个由形式演算（子句连接tableaux）诱导的转移系统，将leanCoP风格搜索和plCoP/rlCoP风格规划统一为有状态策略；使用图神经网络对证明编辑打分，通过模仿学习从已有证明中训练策略；逐步移除搜索脚手架以评估纯学习驱动的性能。

Result: 在M2k、MPTP2078-bushy和TPTP v9.2.1基准上，固定步数预算内，学习策略比leanCoP最多多解决46%的问题，且达到证明的步数减少了一个数量级。

Conclusion: 统一的转移系统接口使得策略学习方法可直接应用于定理证明，学习到的策略在效率和覆盖面上均显著优于传统符号搜索基线，证明了知识驱动的证明策略具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: An automated theorem prover builds a proof step by step, choosing at each point what to add and what to remove. We cast this construction as a policy acting in a transition system induced by a formal calculus, which fixes which steps are sound: for clausal connection tableaux, leanCoP-style search and plCoP/rlCoP-style planning then become stateful policies over one interface, and policy-learning methods apply directly. We equip such policies with a graph neural network that scores proof edits from structure that transfers across problems, train it by imitation learning from found proofs, and measure how performance holds as we remove search scaffolding, from full symbolic backtracking to a policy the network drives alone. Within a fixed step budget on M2k, MPTP2078-bushy, and TPTP v9.2.1, learned policies solve up to 46% more problems than leanCoP, and reach proofs in an order of magnitude fewer steps.

</details>


### [248] [Trace Integrity for LLM Data Agents: A Vision for Auditable Structured Reasoning in Real-World Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26036)
*Srimonti Dutta,Akshata Kishore Moharir*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Trace Integrity（追踪完整性）作为LLM数据代理的部署可靠性评估标准，弥补仅依赖答案准确率评估的不足，并通过CAIT Rate指标量化"答案正确但追踪无效"的静默失败风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构化数据任务中，LLM数据代理可能通过无效的执行轨迹产生正确的基准答案，导致仅评估答案准确率会掩盖计算不可靠的问题。自然语言推理和自由格式的理由无法可靠地指定真实系统所需的算子级程序，形成了"结构鸿沟"（Structure Gap），这使得追踪完整性评估成为必要。

Method: 定义了追踪完整性的七个标准（显式、可执行、模式有效、算子忠实、可重放、答案一致、可审计），并通过执行契约（execution contracts）将其操作化。执行契约将用户意图绑定到模式元素、算子计划、假设、可执行查询、验证状态和最终答案关联。同时引入CAIT（正确答案/无效追踪）率来量化仅用答案评估时计数器不支持输出为成功的频率。

Result: 在BIRD Mini-Dev基准上，直接SQL、操作摘要+SQL和契约优先SQL的答案准确率分别为20%、22%和24%，而追踪完整性通过率分别为39%、43%和40%，CAIT率分别高达55%、59.1%和45.8%。这证明答案准确率、追踪有效性和静默失败风险是三个不同的评估信号。

Conclusion: 真实世界的LLM数据代理不仅应评估其输出是否匹配参考答案，更应评估其输出是否有可审计的计算作为支撑。追踪完整性应作为部署可靠性标准纳入评估体系。

Abstract: Answer accuracy is an insufficient reliability signal for LLM data agents. In structured-data tasks, a benchmark-correct answer can be produced by an invalid trace. This paper introduces Trace Integrity, a deployment reliability criterion for evaluating whether the computation recorded behind an answer is explicit, executable, schema-valid, operator-faithful, replayable, answer-consistent, and auditable. We identify the Structure Gap as the deployment failure mode that makes Trace Integrity necessary: natural-language reasoning and free-form rationales do not reliably specify the operator-level programs required by real-world systems. We operationalize Trace Integrity with execution contracts, structured artifacts that bind user intent to schema elements, operator plans, assumptions, executable queries, verification status, and final-answer linkage. We also introduce CAIT (Correct Answer / Invalid Trace) Rate, which measures how often answer-only evaluation counts computationally unsupported outputs as successes. In an empirical demonstration on BIRD Mini-Dev, Direct SQL, Operation Summary + SQL, and Contract-First SQL achieve answer accuracies of 20%, 22%, and 24%, while their Trace Integrity Pass Rates are 39%, 43%, and 40% and their CAIT Rates remain high at 55%, 59.1%, and 45.8%, showing that answer accuracy, trace validity, and silent-failure risk are distinct evaluation signals. Real-world LLM data agents should, therefore, be evaluated not only by whether their outputs match a reference answer, but by whether those outputs are backed by auditable computation.

</details>


### [249] [SwarmWorld: Stigmergic technological evolution in societies of language-model agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26081)
*Subhadeep Pal,Fiona Y. Wang,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了同质化LLM代理能否在没有预定义角色或集中式工作流的情况下，通过共享环境自发组织形成技术社会。在SwarmWorld环境中，代理通过探索、资源处理、材料测试和构件持久化等行为自组织成演化技术社会，其构建的可执行控制器在代理被移除后由确定性模拟器评估。结果显示，共享社会能发展出比最佳孤立的best-of-N搜索基线更广泛、更具韧性的技术组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数多智能体系统依赖直接对话、预定义角色或集中式工作流，尚不清楚去中心化智能体能否自主构建功能性技术并超越独立搜索的性能。研究旨在验证语言模型代理能否通过共享环境中的局部行动积累，产生持久的集体智能和技术创新。

Method: 采用SwarmWorld环境，使初始同质的LLM代理无需指定角色或配方即可自我组织。代理在空间环境中探索、处理资源、测试材料、构建持久化构件，并编写可执行控制器，由确定性模拟器在不预见干扰下评估。研究将认知与结果分离：代理在固定行动和材料模式内提出架构和控制器，模拟世界决定功能。同时引入显式文化机制来放大协作。

Result: 共享社会产生比强best-of-N孤立搜索基线更广泛、更 resilient 的技术组合，但孤立搜索在最强构件方面仍有竞争力。代理分化为勘探、建设、维护和协调等行为，并随世界成熟而转换。技术通过协作构建、可执行继承和持久代理-构件网络积累，多数复用始于物理观察而非通信。显式文化机制放大协作和组织的功能益处取决于结果和时标。

Conclusion: 物理stigmergy（环境间接协作）本身就能支持有能力的社会，而交互驱动的是持久的技术生态而非普遍优越的个人发明。这证明去中心化LLM代理可以通过共享环境自组织形成技术社会，为集体智能研究提供了新范式。

Abstract: Collective intelligence can emerge when individuals coordinate through a shared environment, allowing local actions to accumulate into durable social organization. Language-model agents offer a new substrate for this process, yet most multi-agent systems rely on direct conversation, predefined roles, or centralized workflows. It remains unclear whether decentralized agents can build functional technologies and outperform independent search. Here, initially homogeneous LLM agents in SwarmWorld self-organize without assigned roles or recipes into evolving technological societies. Agents explore a spatial environment, process resources, test materials, construct persistent artifacts, and write executable controllers evaluated by a deterministic simulator under unseen disturbances after the agents are removed. SwarmWorld splits cognition from consequence: agents propose architectures and controllers within fixed action and material schemas, while the simulated world determines function. Shared societies develop broader, more resilient technological portfolios than a strong best-of-N isolated-search baseline, although isolated search remains competitive for the strongest artifact. Agents differentiate into exploration, construction, maintenance, and coordination behaviors, transitioning as the world matures. Technologies accumulate through collaborative construction, executable inheritance, and persistent agent-artifact networks, with most reuse beginning through physical observation rather than communication. Explicit cultural mechanisms amplify collaboration and organization, but functional benefits depend on outcome and timescale. Physical stigmergy alone supports capable societies, while interaction drives persistent technological ecologies rather than universally superior individual inventions.

</details>


### [250] [Planetary Prediction Engine: Autonomous Geospatial Prediction via Intelligent Data Selection and Foundation Model Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26088)
*Evelyn Ma,Rama Kumar Pasumarthi,Kishwar Shafin,Mandar Sharma,Mimi Sun,Hamed Sadeghi,Dav M. Ebengo,Mbulayi Onesime,Rouslan Solomakhin,John Wamburu,William Ogallo,Aisha Walcott-Bryant,Sanxing Chen,Arbaaz Muslim,Yael Mayer,Ronald Ho,Roy Lee,Ruth Alcantara,Abdoulaye Diack,Monica Bharel,Lambert Rosique,Jeremy Amez-Droz,Christopher Haire,James Manyika,Yossi Matias,Niv Efron,Gautam Prasad,Shravya Shetty*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出行星预测引擎（PPE），一个从自然语言查询出发、端到端自动完成数据检索、多模态融合和模型选择的自主AI系统，在多个地理空间预测任务上显著超越现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建高保真度的行星尺度预测模型面临碎片化数据生态的瓶颈，需要大量人工进行数据检索、多模态数据整理融合和迭代模型选择，技术门槛高且效率低下。

Method: PPE是一个自主AI系统，直接从自然语言查询执行端到端工作流：实时合成多模态数据集，从开放网络和地球观测平台（Data Commons、Google Earth Engine）检索时空相关协变量，并将其与地理空间基础模型嵌入（PDFM、AlphaEarth）融合；同时自动搜索任务定制模型架构族，并配备自动化过拟合防护机制。

Result: PPE在多种任务和领域中一致优于最先进或人工调优的专家基线：美国空间回归中，21个CDC健康指标的R²从60.0%提升至76.8%，FEMA国家风险指数从60.0%提升至64.9%，社会脆弱性指数从58.6%提升至66.2%；尼日利亚粮食安全指标空间降尺度中R²从31.5%翻倍至66.1%；2026年刚果（金）Ebola疫情预报中Recall@10达到83.3%，比公开最先进模型高10.3个百分点。

Conclusion: PPE将自主多模态行星数据发现与针对性模型优化相结合，降低了行星尺度分析的技术门槛，可实现快速、定制化、专家级的部署。

Abstract: Addressing critical global challenges, from food security and disaster risk to disease outbreaks and socio-economic vulnerability, demands high-fidelity geospatial modeling. However, building predictive planetary models remains bottlenecked by a fragmented data ecosystem, requiring manual data retrieval, multimodal data curation and fusion along with iterative model selection. We present the Planetary Prediction Engine (PPE), an autonomous AI system that executes this end-to-end workflow directly from natural-language queries. PPE synthesizes multimodal datasets on the fly, retrieving spatiotemporally relevant covariates across open-web and Earth observation platforms (Data Commons, Google Earth Engine) and fusing them with geospatial foundation model embeddings (PDFM, AlphaEarth). Simultaneously, it searches over task-tailored model architecture families with automated overfitting guards. Across diverse tasks, geographies, and scientific domains, PPE consistently outperforms state-of-the-art or manually tuned expert baselines. For US spatial regression, PPE improves mean $R^2$ across 21 CDC health indicators (76.8% vs. 60.0%), FEMA national risk indices (64.9% vs. 60.0%), and the Social Vulnerability Index (66.2% vs. 58.6%). For spatial downscaling in data-scarce settings, PPE integrates localized proxies to double baseline accuracy in Nigerian food security indicators ($R^2$ of 66.1% vs. 31.5%). For epidemiological nowcasting of the 2026 DRC Bundibugyo Ebola outbreak, PPE achieves a Recall@10 of 83.3% (identifying 15 of 18 newly invaded health zones across five weekly forecasts), a +10.3 percentage-point improvement over the public state-of-the-art modeling (~73%). By combining autonomous multimodal planetary data discovery with targeted model optimization, PPE lowers the technical barrier to planetary-scale analytics, enabling rapid, customized, expert-level deployment.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [251] [GaussVLA: Geometry-Aware Spatial Reasoning for Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.24959)
*Md Selim Sarowar,Md Tanvir Islam,Sungho Kim,Sangtae Ahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GaussVLA，一种基于Mamba的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过高斯空间分词器（GST）将语义和深度特征提升为紧凑的3D高斯令牌，并结合深度感知链式思考（DA-CoT）进行非自回归几何推理。在LIBERO基准上以仅200M参数实现93.5%平均成功率，空间任务达到100%，比SpatialVLA相对平均成功率提升19.7%且参数效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型将视觉观测编码为平面2D补丁令牌，缺乏固有的几何结构；而密集单目深度注入的仅是逐像素标量值，既不编码表面朝向也不包含几何置信度，导致策略在动作预测中的结构化空间推理能力受限。

Method: 提出GaussVLA，包含两个核心模块：1）高斯空间分词器（GST），将冻结的语义和深度特征提升为紧凑的3D高斯令牌，并使用学习查询池化几何显著区域；2）深度感知链式思考（DA-CoT），在语言和流程时间条件下执行结构化的非自回归几何推理。

Result: 在模拟和真实世界评估中均展现强大的空间操作性能且参数高效。LIBERO上平均成功率93.5%，空间套件成功率达100%，仅用200M参数，相对SpatialVLA平均成功率提升19.7%，同时参数效率显著更高。

Conclusion: 通过将2D平面视觉令牌提升为3D高斯表示并进行结构化的深度感知推理，GaussVLA有效解决了VLA模型空间推理能力不足的问题，在性能与参数效率之间取得了优异平衡。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models encode visual observations as flat 2D patch tokens that carry no intrinsic geometric structure, and augmenting them with dense monocular depth injects per-pixel scalar values that encode neither surface orientation nor geometric confidence. This leaves the policy with limited structured spatial reasoning for action prediction. We propose GaussVLA, a Mamba-based VLA that incorporates two custom modules: Gaussian Spatial Tokenizer (GST) to lift frozen semantic and depth features into compact 3D Gaussian tokens, pools geometrically salient regions with learned queries, and \emph{Depth-Aware Chain-of-Thought (DA-CoT)} that performs structured, non-autoregressive geometric reasoning under language and flow-time conditioning. Across both simulation and real-world evaluations, GaussVLA demonstrates strong spatial-manipulation performance while remaining parameter-efficient. On LIBERO, it achieves 93.5% average success and 100.0% success on the Spatial suite with only 200M parameters, improving over SpatialVLA by 19.7% relative average success while remaining significantly more parameter-efficient.

</details>


### [252] [ROS2 Connect: A new ROS2 over WAN Solution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25102)
*Daniel Schott,Lakshminarasimhan Srinivasan,Christian Herrmann,Andreas Nüchter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了ROS2 Connect，一个基于WebSocket的通信框架，使ROS2能够通过路由网络进行透明、安全的远程交互，无需修改网络基础设施或DDS配置。在真实WAN环境下测试显示，其延迟、稳定性和可扩展性均优于DDS Router、rosbridge和Zenoh等现有方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROS2广泛用于分布式机器人系统，但其基于DDS和RTPS的通信架构依赖组播发现机制，在广域网(WAN)环境中通常不可用，导致远程操作面临挑战。

Method: 提出基于WebSocket的客户端-服务器架构，支持主题、服务、动作和系统数据的双向交换，并集成认证与访问控制机制。无需修改网络基础设施或DDS配置即可实现透明交互。

Result: 在真实WAN连接上的实验评估表明，相比DDS Router、rosbridge和Zenoh等现有解决方案，ROS2 Connect具有显著更低的延迟、更高的稳定性和更好的可扩展性。

Conclusion: ROS2 Connect为广域网上的远程操作和分布式机器人应用提供了可靠的通信基础。

Abstract: The Robot Operating System 2 (ROS2) has become a widely adopted framework for the development of distributed robotic systems. However, its communication architecture, based on DDS and RTPS, relies on multicast discovery mechanisms that are typically unavailable in wide-area network (WAN) environments, making remote operation challenging. This work presents ROS2 Connect, a WebSocket-based communication framework that enables transparent and secure ROS2 interaction across routed networks without requiring modifications to network infrastructure or DDS configurations. The proposed client-server architecture supports bidirectional exchange of topics, services, actions, and system data while integrating authentication and access control mechanisms. Experimental evaluation over a real WAN connection demonstrates significantly lower latency, higher stability, and improved scalability compared to existing solutions, including DDS Router, rosbridge and Zenoh. Initial results show that ROS2 Connect provides a reliable foundation for teleoperation and distributed robotics applications over wide-area networks.

</details>


### [253] [Extending Ground-Constraint LiDAR-IMU Calibration to Tilted Surfaces in a Continuous-Time Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25135)
*Vassili Korotkine,Pierre Chamoun,Mohammed Ayman Shalaby,James Richard Forbes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的方法，将针对地面车辆的免目标LiDAR-IMU标定扩展到非平坦环境，通过提出不依赖重力与地面法向量共线假设的地面残差，支持在倾斜表面上进行平面运动标定，并在多个数据集上验证了重复性的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面车辆在正常运行时无法充分激励传感器平台，导致标定退化。现有方法假设重力与物理地面法向量共线，仅限于平坦地面场景，无法适用于倾斜地面。

Method: 提出基于地面平面的残差项，该残差不依赖重力与地面法向量共线的假设，使得在倾斜表面上进行平面运动的LiDAR-IMU标定成为可能。

Result: 在Husky地面车辆采集的数据集、M2DGR数据集以及越野车辆数据集上进行了实验验证，结果表明在倾斜地面和平坦地面场景下重复性均有所提升，其中倾斜场景下的改进尤为显著。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将免目标LiDAR-IMU标定扩展到非平坦环境，在倾斜地面场景下显著提升了标定重复性，且实现和实验均已开源。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel method that extends targetless LiDAR-IMU calibration for ground vehicles to non- flat environments. Calibration typically necessitates full exci- tation of the sensor rig, a requirement that is not fulfilled by ground vehicles in normal operation. To address the degenerate planar motion, state-of-the-art methods propose residuals that assume the colinearity of the gravity and physical surface normal vectors, restricting usage to cases where the ground is assumed flat. This paper proposes ground-plane residuals that do not require this assumption, and are applicable for planar motion on a tilted surface. Results are demonstrated on a dataset collected from a Husky ground vehicle, on the M2DGR dataset, as well as on an offroad vehicle dataset. Repeatability is shown to be improved both in tilted and flat-ground scenarios, with strong improvement demonstrated for the tilted case. The implementation and experiments are open-sourced at https://github.com/vkorotkine/licalib_tilted_ground.

</details>


### [254] [SkyDrive: Learning to Drive in a New City from Aerial Traffic Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25142)
*Weijiang Xiong,Lan Feng,Alexandre Alahi,Nikolas Geroliminis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SkyDrive框架，利用无人机空中交通监控作为新环境下自动驾驶轨迹规划器的高效监督数据源。基于137小时航拍视频提取65万条驾驶样本，实验表明30分钟空中监控即可显著缓解跨城市域差距，为自动驾驶系统新城市适配提供了可扩展且高效的数据获取方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶规划器在zero-shot迁移到新环境时因交通规则、道路布局和驾驶行为的域差异而性能严重退化，而传统车辆传感器套件的数据采集成本高昂且效率低。因此需要一种可扩展且高效的替代数据采集方案。

Method: SkyDrive框架利用无人机空中交通监控获取监督信号，航拍平台可在扩展视野内同时观测多个道路使用者，使每辆车都能成为具有真实驾驶行为的数据源。基于137小时航拍视频提取65万条驾驶样本，构建轨迹规划器和运动预测器基准。

Result: 零样本实验显示多个模型存在显著跨城市域差距，但通过有限的空中监督（每个地点约30分钟监控）即可有效缓解这些差距，验证了空中交通监控作为高效可扩展数据源的有效性。

Conclusion: 空中交通监控是自动驾驶系统新城市适配的高效且可扩展的数据来源，能在少量监督下显著改善跨城市域泛化性能，数据与代码将公开发布。

Abstract: Autonomous driving has made remarkable progress through imitation learning with massive human demonstration data. However, a trained planner often degrades severely when applied to a new environment zero-shot, because of domain shifts in traffic regulations, road layout and driving behaviors. Therefore, adapting a trajectory planner to a new city typically requires resource-demanding local data collection with a vehicle sensor suite. In this work, we show that driving behavior can be learned from a scalable and efficient alternative. We introduce \emph{SkyDrive}, a framework that utilizes drone-based traffic monitoring to provide efficient supervision for autonomous driving agents in a new environment. While vehicle-based data collection logs the ego and its surroundings, an aerial platform naturally observes many road users simultaneously over an extended field of view. As a result, every vehicle can be a data source with grounded driving behavior, effectively scaling up the amount of supervision. Based on 137 hours of aerial traffic monitoring footage, we extract 650K driving samples and construct a benchmark for trajectory planners and motion predictors. Zero-shot experiments with multiple models reveal significant cross-city domain gaps, but many of them can be alleviated by limited supervision from the sky, e.g., 30 minutes of monitoring per location. Our findings show that aerial traffic monitoring is an efficient and scalable data source for adapting autonomous driving systems in new cities. Data and code will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [255] [Sequential Object Placement Optimization with Convex Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25162)
*Yuezhe Zhang,Xiangyu Lyu,Sohan Rudra,Davide Tateo,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出SOPO-CD，一种将机器人物体装箱问题转化为可微分非线性优化问题的序列优化框架，消除了传统方法对空间离散化分辨率的依赖。通过证明凸物体在凸包内的放置本质上是对物体顶点施加约束，实现了闭合形式的约束及其导数计算（200ns内），并开发了定制求解器，在毫秒级内完成紧约束空间中的最优放置，相比经典网格搜索方法提速100倍。框架已推广到2D七巧板、2D俄罗斯方块和3D装箱问题，并在真实七巧板拼图场景中用Allegro Hand和Xarm机械臂进行了在线验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人物体装箱因组合搜索的维度灾难以及不规则形状物体的动态和接触约束而极具挑战性。现有启发式和学习方法假设空间离散化分辨率有限，随着离散化精度提高计算效率急剧下降。作者旨在消除这些假设，提出更高效且不依赖空间离散化的优化框架。

Method: 将物体放置建模为在分解自由空间中的可微分非线性优化问题；证明凸物体放置在凸包内等价于约束物体顶点位于凸包内部；约束及其导数可写成闭合形式并在200纳秒内完成计算；实现定制求解器在紧约束空间中毫秒级获得最优放置，并将框架推广至2D和3D多种装箱场景。

Result: 定制求解器在毫秒级内完成紧约束空间中的最优放置，相比经典网格搜索方法实现100倍加速；框架在2D Tangram、2D Tetris和3D Bin Packing上均展现了强大的计算性能和装箱利用率；并成功在真实七巧板拼图任务中通过Allegro Hand和Xarm机械臂进行在线求解演示。

Conclusion: SOPO-CD通过将装箱问题转化为可微分的闭合形式优化问题，有效克服了传统方法对空间离散化的依赖，实现了显著的计算加速和良好的装箱效果，并已验证其在真实机器人系统中的可行性。

Abstract: Robotic object packing has been a core challenge for robotic deployment in logistics, industry, etc., due to the curse of dimensionality in combinatorial search and the difficulty of dealing with dynamic and contact constraints for irregularly shaped objects. Current heuristic and learning-based methods assume a limited spatial discretization resolution of space, and computation becomes extremely inefficient as discretization accuracy increases. In this work, we eliminate these assumptions by introducing SOPO-CD, a sequential optimization framework that frames object placement as a differentiable nonlinear optimization problem in a decomposed free space. We prove that placing a convex object inside a convex hull is essentially constraining the vertices of the object inside the convex hull. The constraints and their derivatives can be written in closed form and calculated within $200$ns. We implement a custom solver that achieves optimal placement within tightly constrained space in milliseconds; a $100 \times$ speedup compared to a classical grid search method. We generalize our framework to 2D Tangram, 2D Tetris, and 3D Bin Packing, and have demonstrated strong computational performance and packing utility. We also demonstrate solving a real-world Tangram puzzle online using an Allegro Hand and an Xarm.

</details>


### [256] [Control-Oriented Learning for Dynamic Tracking and Stability Analysis of Soft Pneumatic Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25171)
*Nithin S. Kumar,Eric J. Barth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向控制的数据驱动建模与控制框架，将软体气动执行器的行为分解为非线性静态平衡模型和线性残差动力学模型（通过EDMDc辨识），实现前馈补偿、任务空间反馈控制以及局部闭环稳定性分析。实验表明低速（约10 mm/s）轨迹跟踪RMSE约1 mm，高速（约100 mm/s）时RMSE低于10 mm，并能稳定跟踪峰值加速度超过25 m/s²的高动态参考轨迹且同时进行实时避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体气动执行器具有固有柔顺性和安全交互特性，但其高度非线性和分布式动力学使其难以建模和控制，现有方法缺乏兼具准确性和可操作性的控制框架。

Method: 将执行器行为分解为非线性静态平衡模型和线性残差动力学模型，其中残差模型使用扩展动态模式分解与控制（EDMDc）辨识；利用该表示实现前馈补偿、任务空间反馈控制，并通过增广线性模型进行局部闭环稳定性分析。

Result: 低速（约10 mm/s）轨迹跟踪RMSE约1 mm；高速（约100 mm/s）时RMSE低于10 mm；峰值加速度超过25 m/s²的高动态参考下仍稳定跟踪，并同时执行实时避障；稳定性分析准确预测了稳定、临界和不稳定运行区域。

Conclusion: 结构化的控制导向学习为软体执行器控制提供了准确且实用的框架，兼具建模精度、实时控制能力和稳定性分析能力。

Abstract: Soft pneumatic actuators offer inherent compliance and safe interaction but remain difficult to model and control because of their highly nonlinear, distributed dynamics. We present a control-oriented data-driven modeling and control framework that decomposes actuator behavior into a nonlinear static equilibrium model and a linear residual dynamics model identified using Extended Dynamic Mode Decomposition with control (EDMDc). This representation enables feedforward compensation, task-space feedback control, and local closed-loop stability analysis through an augmented linear model. Experiments achieve approximately 1 mm root mean square error (RMSE) during low-speed (approximately 10 mm/s) trajectory tracking and below 10 mm RMSE at higher speeds (approximately 100 mm/s). The framework further achieves stable tracking of highly dynamic user-generated references with peak accelerations exceeding 25 m/s^2 while simultaneously performing real-time obstacle avoidance. Finally, the proposed stability analysis is experimentally validated by accurately predicting stable, marginal, and unstable operating regimes. These results demonstrate that structured, control-oriented learning provides an accurate and practical framework for soft actuator control.

</details>


### [257] [CRESSim-Neo: A Batched GPU Simulation Engine for Surgical Robotics and Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25192)
*Yafei Ou,Ahnaf Naheen,Tleukhan Mussin,Hans Jarales,Melwin Moncy,Mahdi Tavakoli*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CRESSim-Neo，一个面向手术机器人和机器人学习的批量GPU仿真引擎，支持刚体、变形组织、流体和绳索的基于位置的仿真，并结合批量渲染、手术专用传感和GPU驻留数据管线。该引擎支持组织操作、液体抽吸、缝合、缆绳驱动机器人和超声图像合成，通过DLPack实现与PyTorch的零拷贝集成。在NVIDIA RTX 4090上，对于8192个并行CartPole环境可达到每秒203万环境步数，并扩展到包含组织变形、流体交互和超声传感的批量手术场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人和机器人学习领域需要一种统一、可扩展且高效的仿真平台，能够同时处理多物理场（刚体、变形组织、流体、绳索）的仿真，并支持大规模并行批处理以加速机器人学习算法训练。

Method: CRESSim-Neo采用基于位置的仿真方法统一模拟刚体、可变形组织、流体和绳索，结合批量GPU渲染、手术专用传感模块和GPU驻留数据管线。通过DLPack协议实现物理和渲染缓冲区与PyTorch的直接零拷贝集成，支持GPU驻留的机器人学习。

Result: 在NVIDIA RTX 4090上：1）8192个并行CartPole环境达到每秒203万环境步数；2）可扩展到包含组织变形、流体交互和超声传感的批量手术场景；3）成功演示了刚体、变形体和流体仿真任务以及视觉和手术机器人学习场景。

Conclusion: CRESSim-Neo为手术仿真、合成数据生成和手术机器人学习提供了一个统一且可扩展的平台，在高吞吐量并行环境仿真和复杂手术场景批处理方面展示了显著优势。

Abstract: We introduce CRESSim-Neo, a batched GPU simulation engine for surgical robotics and robot learning. CRESSim-Neo combines position-based simulation of rigid bodies, deformable tissues, fluids, and strands with batched rendering, surgery-specific sensing, and a GPU-resident data pipeline. The engine supports applications including tissue manipulation, fluid suction, suturing, cable-driven robots, and ultrasound image synthesis. Direct access to physics and rendering buffers enables GPU-resident robot learning and zero-copy PyTorch integration using DLPack. We demonstrate CRESSim-Neo across rigid-body, deformable-body, and fluid simulation tasks, including vision-based and surgical robot-learning scenarios. On an NVIDIA RTX 4090, the engine achieves up to 2.03 million environment steps per second for 8192 parallel CartPole environments, and scales to batched surgical scenarios involving tissue deformation, fluid interaction, and ultrasound sensing. Overall, CRESSim-Neo provides a unified and scalable platform for surgical simulation, synthetic data generation, and surgical robot learning.

</details>


### [258] [Longitudinal Robot Learning from Demonstration with Care Providers in a Home Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25196)
*Nina Moorman,Julianna Schalkwyk,Vriksha Srihari,Qingyu Xiao,Kamel Alrashedy,Hongseok Jeong,Kiersten Lange,Matthew B. Luebbers,Matthew Gombolay*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了非专家终端用户在家庭环境中、没有机器人专家实时反馈的情况下，通过示范教学机器人的主要障碍。实验采用预训练和自适应反馈两种演示者引导方式支持用户，并在多次访问中面向护理人员群体进行，以提高生态效度，最终计划开源所得数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LfD可用性评估多在受控实验室环境中进行且有机器人实验者在场，缺乏对非专家用户在真实家庭环境中独立使用LfD的评估，因此需要识别其关键障碍。

Method: 以护理人员为对象，通过多次访问家庭环境进行人类受试者实验，利用预训练和自适应反馈两种演示者引导方式为非专家用户提供支持，评估其教学机器人的过程。

Result: 识别出非专家终端用户在无实时专家反馈情况下教学机器人的关键障碍，并生成了护理人员多次在家教机器人完成辅助任务的数据集。

Conclusion: 在真实家庭环境中评估LfD可用性具有重要意义，所提出的开放数据集有助于推动该领域进一步研究。

Abstract: Learning from demonstration (LfD) methods enable non-expert end users to teach robots novel skills without explicit programming. However most evaluations of the usability of LfD with non-experts has been conducted in controlled laboratory environments with a robotics experimenter present. In this work we identify non-expert end users' key barriers when teaching robots via demonstration without live robotics expert feedback in a home environment. In our human subjects experiment we support the non-expert end users through two forms of demonstrator guidance developed in prior work: pre-training and adaptive feedback. Towards the ecological validity of the evaluation, we conduct this experimentation over multiple visits, with a population of care providers. Finally, we propose to open source the resulting LfD dataset of care providers teaching a robot assistive tasks over multiple visits to a home environment.

</details>


### [259] [Development of a Voice-Controlled Tendon-Driven Bionic Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25222)
*Urja Kohli,Shagata Chanda,Kritika Gandhi,Charu Nigam,Aditi Surya Kamal,Pooja Bhati*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计并实现了一种基于肌腱驱动的自动化仿生手，通过Arduino微控制器和伺服电机实现手指协调运动，支持手势执行和蓝牙语音控制，实验表明系统具有稳定的抓取能力和可重复的控制精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手部功能受损会严重影响个体的日常生活能力，因此设计稳定且可控的辅助支撑装置是一个重要的研究领域。

Method: 采用肌腱驱动方法，通过伺服电机配合角度标定控制产生手指运动；使用基于Arduino的微控制器编程执行预设手势（张手、握拳、捏取和半屈曲）；通过蓝牙语音指令接口实现交互，将语音命令即时转换为手指动作。

Result: 手指完全屈曲所需总时间约为7-8秒；系统在多个致动周期中表现出可重复且恒定的运动，无张力或控制精度损失；对不同形状和大小的物体均能稳定抓取，手指间保持一致的协调性。

Conclusion: 所提出的系统提供了可预测且稳定的控制行为，具备简单高效的机械与控制架构。

Abstract: The impairment of the hands can seriously affect the abilities of every individual to perform the every-day activity, so the design of stable and controllable support devices is a significant field of study. This paper is about the design and implementation of an automated bionic hand which is dedicated to the coordinated finger movement through the simplified and efficient actuation mechanism. The method that the proposed system was designed on is the tendon-based method whereby the servo motors generate the movement of the fingers, with assistance of the angular control which is calibrated. An actuation is controlled by a microcontroller that will be programmed by use of an Arduino-based microcontroller to carry out programmed gestures that include open hand, fist, pinch and half flexion. It has an interface that is voice command enabled to make it easy to interact with a Bluetooth based sender receiver architecture which offers an option of executing trained commands which are immediately converted to finger actions. To explore the motions behavior, finger coordination and control response to the input, the behavior of the experiment system is tested. The actuation of the fingers was found to take a total of about 7-8 seconds to achieve full flexion of all fingers in a sequence. The system showed repetitive and constant motion throughout several actuation cycles without loss of any apparent tension or precision of control. There was a stable grasp of objects of different shapes and sizes, which implied consistent coordination between the fingers. These findings indicate that the proposed system offers predictable and steady control behavior and has a simple and efficient mechanical and control architecture.

</details>


### [260] [Generative Action-Chunk Sampling for Adaptive Stiffness Control in Physical Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25284)
*Aoi Otake,Ferdinand Hartmann,Ko Igari,Shingo Murata*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于生成式动作块采样的自适应刚度框架，通过采样动作块之间的变异性来动态调节机器人关节刚度与阻尼，在物理人机协作中实现"意图明确时提供辅助、意图模糊时保持顺从"的平衡。在四方向协作搬运任务中，该方法成功率0.95，优于固定刚度基线（0.83）和确定性基线（0.69）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理人机协作需要在人类意图明确时提供坚定辅助，而在未来动作有多种可能性时保持顺从。现有方法通常采用固定刚度策略，无法根据人类意图的不确定性动态调整机器人的柔顺程度，导致辅助与顺从之间的平衡难以实现。

Method: 该方法基于生成式动作块采样构建自适应刚度框架。策略以RGB图像和外部关节力矩估计为条件，从观测条件先验中采样多个未来动作块，利用采样动作块之间的变异性（方差）来连续调节关节刚度与阻尼：变异性大时降低刚度以顺从人类引导，变异性小时提高刚度以提供更坚定的辅助。

Result: 在真实世界四方向协作搬运任务中，该方法平均成功率达0.95，明显优于固定刚度消融实验（0.83）和确定性基线（0.69）。在方向接近确定时，采样动作块间的变异性增大，控制器相应地降低了刚度，验证了该方法能有效利用动作采样变异性作为在线控制信号。

Conclusion: 生成式策略采样的动作变异性可以作为一种有效的在线控制信号，用于平衡物理人机交互中的辅助与顺从，为自适应刚度控制提供了一种新的范式。

Abstract: Physical human-robot collaboration requires a robot to provide assistance when human intention is clear while remaining compliant when several future motions are plausible. We present an adaptive stiffness framework based on generative action-chunk sampling. Conditioned on an RGB image and external joint-torque estimates, the policy samples multiple future action chunks from an observation-conditioned prior. Variation among the sampled action chunks is used to continuously adapt joint stiffness and damping. Greater variation makes the robot more compliant to facilitate human guidance, whereas lower variation provides firmer assistance. In a real-world collaborative transport task with four possible directions, the proposed method achieved an average success rate of 0.95, compared with 0.83 for a fixed-stiffness ablation and 0.69 for a deterministic baseline. Near direction determination, variation among the sampled action chunks increased and the controller accordingly reduced stiffness. These results suggest that variation among actions sampled by a generative policy can serve as an online control signal for balancing assistance and compliance in physical human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [261] [RAEM: Robust Autonomous Exploration for Multi-Floor Environments with a Quadruped Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25366)
*Zikang Yuan,Yuan Ren,Yian Wang,Yixue Wang,Enze Fang,Xuewei Zhang,Junda Cheng,Chi Chen,Chin-Pang Ho,Lijun Zhu,Shaohang Xu,Kwang-Ting Cheng,Xin Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出RAEM，一个面向四足机器人在多楼层环境中进行鲁棒自主探索的框架。它采用混合局部-全局可遍历性表示（局部断层扫描图+局部3D网格图+全局拓扑图），并引入楼梯中心对齐策略和双路径搜索机制，在仿真和实际实验中实现了包括五层楼梯在内的多楼层结构连续探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地面机器人探索方法大多依赖平面可遍历性表示，无法充分表达多楼层建筑的重叠结构和跨楼层连通性；基于断层扫描的表示虽有效但全局建图维护计算开销大；楼梯间稀疏碎片化的LiDAR观测会降低局部可遍历性估计，导致视点放置不规则和暂时性拓扑断连。

Method: RAEM采用混合局部-全局可遍历性表示：局部维护断层扫描图和分类的3D网格图以进行在线地形分析和连通性评估；全局通过增量构建带高程信息的拓扑图实现跨楼层探索规划。此外引入楼梯中心对齐策略以减少攀爬时的突转偏航，以及双路径搜索机制以在全局拓扑局部断连时恢复引导路径。

Result: 广泛的仿真和真实世界实验表明，RAEM在多楼层结构中实现了鲁棒且计算稳定的自主探索，包括对五层楼梯的连续探索。

Conclusion: RAEM通过混合局部-全局表示和针对性策略，有效解决了多楼层环境中四足机器人探索的计算开销、可遍历性估计退化以及拓扑断连等问题，验证了其在实际多楼层场景中的可行性和稳定性。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose RAEM, a robust autonomous exploration framework for quadruped robots operating in multi-floor environments. Most existing ground-robot exploration approaches rely on planar traversability representations, which cannot adequately represent the overlapping structures and cross-floor connectivity of multi-floor buildings. Although tomography-based representations provide effective traversability modeling for multi-floor navigation, maintaining a global tomography map incurs substantial computational overhead for online exploration with frequent replanning. Moreover, sparse and fragmented LiDAR observations in stairwells can degrade local traversability estimation, leading to irregular viewpoint placement and temporary topological disconnections. To address these challenges, RAEM adopts a hybrid local-global traversability representation, in which a local tomography map and an explicitly categorized local 3D grid map are used for online terrain analysis and connectivity evaluation, while an elevation-aware global topological graph is incrementally constructed from these local spatial representations for efficient cross-floor exploration planning. We further introduce a staircase center alignment strategy to reduce abrupt yaw variations during climbing and a dual path searching mechanism to recover guidance paths when the global topology is locally disconnected. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate robust and computationally stable autonomous exploration across multi-floor structures, including continuous exploration of a five-floor stairwell.

</details>


### [262] [A Taxonomy of Construction Task Activities for Robot Workers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25395)
*Sadman Sakib,Zhangyi None Peng,Yujie Pang,Yu Otsuki,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TARCAT是一个基于职业的机器人操作分类体系，从91个O*NET任务和30个教学视频中提取，定义了41个动作原语、12个组和3个类别，可用于组织演示、指定机器人需求并支持编码代理构建技能库，并在DOBOT CR3机械臂上进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期视觉-语言-动作模型为机器人提供了更广泛的操作范式，但建筑场景部署需要精确的工人活动清单及所需能力。现有系统缺乏一个可解释、职业化的分类体系来系统性组织和描述建筑作业中的机器人操作任务。

Method: 从7个高就业建筑职业的91个O*NET任务和30个教学视频中提取分类体系，定义41个动作原语，分为12个组和3个类别，并通过参数化原语序列组合成可复用技能。

Result: 构建了TARCAT分类体系，提供可解释的结构来组织演示、指定机器人需求，并在DOBOT CR3机械臂搭配CRAFT手上验证了选定的动作原语。

Conclusion: TARCAT为分析人类工作和开发通用建筑机器人提供了通用词汇表，有助于弥合人类行为分析系统与机器人执行系统之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Recent vision-language-action models offer a path toward robots with broader repertoires than conventional task-specific systems. Construction deployment, however, requires a precise inventory of worker activities and the capabilities needed to execute them. We present TARCAT, an occupation-grounded taxonomy derived from 91 O*NET tasks across seven high-employment construction occupations and 30 instructional videos of physical work. TARCAT defines 41 action primitives in 12 groups and three classes and provides a mechanism for composing parameterized primitive sequences into reusable skills. This human-interpretable structure can organize demonstrations, specify robot requirements, and support coding agents that retrieve and extend skill libraries. We also demonstrate selected primitives on a DOBOT CR3 arm with a CRAFT hand. TARCAT thereby provides a common vocabulary for analyzing human work and developing general-purpose construction robots. Annotations are available at https://github.com/AICPS/TARCAT-Taxonomy.

</details>


### [263] [LAC: Linear and Angular Compliance for Humanoid Whole-body Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25405)
*Yang Liu,Zhongkai Gu,Wei Zhu,Mitsuhiro Hayashibe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出LAC，一种通用全身控制器，能够同时实现上身受到外力/力矩时的线性与角度柔顺响应，通过大规模增强数据集和师生强化学习训练单一策略，在仿真和真实世界实验中验证了全身柔顺控制效果及遥操作移动操作任务的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的人形机器人任务涉及与物体和人类的物理交互，但现有控制器要么将外力视为扰动加以拒绝，要么将柔顺限制在有限的身体连杆上且忽略角度效应，缺乏对全身线性与角度柔顺控制的通用方案。

Method: 首先将全身柔顺响应合成为大规模增强数据集：从人类交互数据中提取接触帧并施加采样力与力偶事件，在每个接触连杆上，外力与被动屈服的运动学链产生的虚拟力矩在指令刚度下驱动虚拟导纳。随后通过师生强化学习训练单一策略以在外部力/力矩作用下跟踪柔顺运动。

Result: 大量仿真和真实实验表明：对上身各处施加力/力矩时能实现全身柔顺响应，刚度指令在整个范围内可单调调节，且适用于遥操作移动操作任务。

Conclusion: LAC作为一种通用全身控制器，同时实现了指令化的线性与角度柔顺，为人形机器人的物理交互任务提供了有效的控制方案。

Abstract: Real-world humanoid tasks involve physical interaction with objects and humans, yet current controllers either reject external forces as disturbances or restrict compliance to limited body links while ignoring angular effects. We present LAC, a general whole-body controller that simultaneously realizes commanded Linear and Angular Compliance for wrenches applied to the upper body. First, we synthesize whole-body compliant responses into a large-scale augmented dataset. Sampled force and couple events are imposed on contact frames extracted from human interaction data. At each contact link, the external force and a virtual torque from the passively yielding kinematic chain drive a virtual admittance under the commanded stiffness. Subsequently, teacher-student reinforcement learning trains a single policy to track the compliant motions under external wrenches. Finally, extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate whole-body compliant responses to wrenches across the upper body, monotonic modulation over the full range of both stiffness commands, and applicability to teleoperated loco-manipulation tasks. Project website: https://lac-humanoid.github.io/

</details>


### [264] [SUPER ODOMETRY 2.0: Resilient Odometry via Hierarchical Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25427)
*Shibo Zhao,Sifan Zhou,Yuchen Zhang,Ji Zhang,Chen Wang,Wenshan Wang,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Super Odometry，一个自适应传感器融合框架，通过层次化结构集成四个核心模块（自适应特征选择、自适应状态方向选择、自适应引擎选择和基于学习的惯性里程计），在烟雾、沙尘暴、雪天或低光等极端退化环境下实现稳健的机器人里程计，并在200公里和800小时的多种机器人平台验证中展现出卓越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有里程计系统在严重传感退化（如烟雾、沙尘暴、雪天、低光照）和极端条件下往往失效，威胁自主系统的安全性与功能性，亟需一种能够动态适应不同环境退化程度的稳健里程计方案。

Method: Super Odometry采用层次化传感器融合框架，从低级到高级集成四个核心模块：自适应特征选择、自适应状态方向选择、自适应引擎选择，以及一种新型基于学习的惯性里程计。该惯性里程计基于超过100小时异构机器人平台数据训练，并将IMU提升到与相机和LiDAR同等重要地位，在外部传感器失效时提供可靠后备。

Result: 该方法在200公里行驶距离和800小时运行时间的多种平台（空中、轮式、腿式机器人）上得到验证，覆盖不同传感器配置、环境退化和激进运动模式，证明了其在全退化环境中的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Super Odometry标志着向全退化环境中安全、长期机器人自主性迈出的重要一步，通过动态适应环境退化程度和强化IMU在融合框架中的地位，显著提升了极端条件下的里程计可靠性。

Abstract: Resilient and robust odometry is crucial for autonomous systems operating in complex and dynamic environments. Existing odometry systems often struggle with severe sensory degradations and extreme conditions such as smoke, sandstorms, snow, or low-light conditions, threatening both the safety and functionality of robots. To address these challenges, we present Super Odometry, a sensor fusion framework that dynamically adapts to varying levels of environmental degradation. Super Odometry employs a hierarchical structure to integrate four core modules from lower-level to higher-level adaptability including adaptive feature selection, adaptive state direction selection, adaptive engine selection, and a novel learning- based inertial odometry. The inertial odometry, trained on over 100 hours of heterogeneous robotic platforms, captures comprehensive motion dynamics. Super Odometry elevates the inertial measurement unit (IMU) to equal importance with camera and LiDAR within the sensor fusion framework, providing a reliable fallback when exteroceptive sensors fail. Super Odometry has been validated across 200 kilometers and 800 operational hours on a fleet of aerial, wheeled, and legged robots, under diverse sensor configurations, environmental degradation, and aggressive motion profiles. It marks an important step towards safe and long-term robotic autonomy in all-degraded environments.

</details>


### [265] [Towards safe and optimal flight: Viability Kernel MPC for Fully Actuated Multirotor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25459)
*Massimiliano Bertoni,Alberto Piccina,Gianni Lunardi,Elias Fontanari,Andrea Del Prete,Angelo Cenedese,Giulia Michieletto*

Main category: cs.RO
